Canonical Verification Report: DOST-SEI 
Graduate Scholarship Programs 
A systemic audit of the live scholarship database (public.scholarships, export timestamp 
2026-08-05) reveals structural inaccuracies, incomplete data models, and critical logic 
mismatches between live database parameters and official agency mandates governing 
graduate funding. In a production-grade scholarship matching engine, loose data typing and 
qualitative assumptions generate severe failure modes. False-positive recommendations direct 
ineligible applicants to submit applications for programs that will reject them, while 
false-negative omissions suppress viable funding opportunities for qualified candidates. 
This report provides canonical verification for five Department of Science and Technology - 
Science Education Institute (DOST-SEI) graduate scholarship programs: the master umbrella 
entry (ID 3) and its four component sub-tracks—Accelerated Science and Technology Human 
Resource Development Program (ASTHRDP, ID 133), Engineering Research and Development 
for Technology (ERDT, ID 134), Capacity Building Program in Science and Mathematics 
Education (CBPSME, ID 135), and Science and Technology Education for Regional 
Development (STRAND, ID 136). 
Data verification strictly follows a four-tiered authority hierarchy: Tier 1 comprises direct 
government web portals (.gov.ph), official provider systems (science-scholarships.ph), official 
downloadable PDF guidelines, and governing administrative orders. Tier 2 covers official press 
releases and advisories published directly by governing secretariats. Tier 3 includes trusted 
university scholarship notices used solely to locate primary sources, while Tier 4 unverified 
blogs and secondary aggregators are discarded. Fields lacking explicit mandates in Tier 1 
sources are designated as NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE.

DOST-SEI Graduate Scholarship Programs 
(ASTHRDP/CBPSME/ERDT/STRAND) (ID: 3) 


--- PAGE 3 ---
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: DOST-SEI Graduate Scholarship Programs (Umbrella Entry covering 
ASTHRDP, CBPSME, ERDT, and STRAND) 
●​ Provider: Department of Science and Technology - Science Education Institute 
(DOST-SEI) 
●​ Category: Government / National / Graduate / Merit-based 
●​ Official Website: https://www.science-scholarships.ph/ 
●​ Application Portal: https://www.science-scholarships.ph/ 
●​ Official Guidelines: DOST-SEI Local Graduate Scholarship Guidelines & Application 
E-Forms 
●​ Current Status: Active 
2. Purpose 
To boost the production of high-level human resources needed for Research and Development 
(R&D) in Science and Technology (S&T), upgrade engineering research capability, improve the 
quality of science and mathematics education, and strengthen the faculty profile of regional 
universities across the Philippines. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen. 
●​ Residency: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Education Level: Graduate (Master's Degree and Doctoral/PhD Degree). 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: MS Program: Years 1 and 2; PhD Program: Years 1, 2, and 3 (up to 
Year 4 for straight-PhD track). 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No (Lateral entry ongoing graduate students with earned units 
are eligible). 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible for undergraduate degree enrollment; applicants 
must possess a completed Bachelor's degree (for MS) or Master's degree (for PhD). 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes (Incoming 1st year graduate students and regular ongoing 
MS/PhD students). 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must be admitted or enrolled as a regular graduate 
student in an approved priority S&T program at a DOST-SEI consortium member 
university. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Must meet the admission requirements for graduate studies at 
the accepting consortium university. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Governed by host university 
graduate school admission and retention standards; live database entry of 88% is an 
unverified artifact). 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Uncapped / No parental or 
personal income limit is imposed in official DOST graduate guidelines; live database cap 
of ₱500,000 is incorrect). 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted to designated DOST-SEI Graduate Scholarship 


--- PAGE 4 ---
Consortium Member Universities (e.g., NSC, ERDT, CBPSME, and STRAND consortia). 
●​ Priority Courses: DOST-SEI Priority S&T Fields (Natural Sciences, Engineering, Science 
& Mathematics Education, Agriculture, Computer Science, etc.). 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Educator status for CBPSME; Regional HEI faculty status for 
STRAND. 
●​ Good Moral: Required (Validated via NBI Clearance or Certificate of Good Moral 
Character). 
●​ Health Requirements: Physically and mentally fit to study, as certified by a licensed 
physician. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must be a full-time student; must NOT be employed or practicing a 
profession while on scholarship, or must secure an official approved leave of absence 
(LOA) from the employer; must not enjoy concurrent government or private scholarships; 
must execute a service contract. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Graduating college seniors entering graduate school, BS/BA degree 
holders applying for MS, MS degree holders applying for PhD, and regular ongoing 
MS/PhD students. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate freshmen; incoming 1st-year 
graduate students may apply. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate sophomores; ongoing 
2nd-year MS/PhD graduate students may apply. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate juniors. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Graduating college seniors entering an MS program upon 
graduation may apply. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes (Bachelor's graduates for MS; Master's graduates for PhD). 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Announced per academic cycle by consortium universities (typically 
March/April for 1st Semester; September/October for 2nd Semester). 
●​ Closing Date: Varies by university (e.g., June 30 for 1st Semester intake). 
●​ Application Cycle: Semester / Term intake. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–2027. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Full actual tuition and other school fees. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: ₱30,000.00 per month for MS scholars; ₱38,000.00 per month for PhD 
scholars. 
●​ Allowance: Learning Materials and/or Connectivity Allowance of ₱20,000.00 per 
Academic Year. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into the Learning Materials and Connectivity Allowance 
(₱20,000.00/AY). 
●​ Transportation: Actual one (1) economy class roundtrip fare per Academic Year (for 
scholars studying outside their home province). 


--- PAGE 5 ---
●​ Insurance: Full Group Personal Accident Insurance coverage. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Research Allowance: Student Research Support Fund (SRSF): Outright 
Thesis/Dissertation Allowance of ₱60,000.00 (MS) or ₱100,000.00 (PhD); Research 
Grant up to ₱114,000.00 (MS) or ₱253,000.00 (PhD); Dissemination Grant up to 
₱75,000.00 (MS) or ₱150,000.00 (PhD); Mentor's Fee of ₱36,000.00 (MS) or ₱72,000.00 
(PhD). 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (10% of thesis 
allowance is released upon submission of final hardbound copies and graduation 
requirements). 
●​ Return Service: Mandatory return service obligation in the Philippines on a full-time basis 
immediately after degree completion, equivalent to one (1) year of service for every year 
of scholarship availed. 
●​ Other Benefits: Career Incentive Program (CIP) eligibility upon graduation with Graduate 
Fellow positions (MS: ₱59,802/month; PhD: ₱82,098/month); MECO-TECO Sandwich 
Scholarship Program research exchange eligibility. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Duly accomplished DOST-SEI Graduate Scholarship Information Sheet / Application 
Form. 
2.​ PSA Birth Certificate (Photocopy). 
3.​ Certified True Copy of Official Transcript of Records (TOR) for BS (and MS if applying for 
PhD). 
4.​ Official Endorsement Forms from two (2) former college professors (for MS) or two (2) 
former MS professors (for PhD). 
5.​ Medical Certificate from a licensed physician stating the applicant is physically and 
mentally fit to study. 
6.​ Valid NBI Clearance. 
7.​ Official Letter of Admission with regular status from the Program Head of the accepting 
institution. 
8.​ Approved Program of Study (POS). 
9.​ If employed: Official recommendation and approved Leave of Absence (LOA) from the 
head of agency, or proof of resignation. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Must maintain a satisfactory General Weighted Average (GWA/GPA) 
prescribed by the host graduate school each term. 
●​ Regular Load: Must carry a full academic load per semester based on the approved 
Program of Study (9–12 units regular term, 3–6 units midyear). 
●​ No Failures: Must have no failing, incomplete, or dropped grades in coursework. 
●​ Return Service: Execution of scholarship agreement binding scholar to return service. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Employment or professional practice while on scholarship without an approved official 
leave of absence. 


--- PAGE 6 ---
●​ Failure to maintain academic retention standards or receiving a failing grade in any 
graduate subject. 
●​ Concurrent enjoyment of another government or private scholarship covering the same 
degree program. 
●​ Failure to comply with the return service obligation or non-completion of the degree 
program without valid justification (requires full financial refund with interest).

13. Matching Risks 
●​ Corrupted Financial Parameters: Live database ID 3 sets max_income: 500000 and 
allowance_mo: 15000. The matching engine must remove the income cap (as DOST 
graduate grants are uncapped) and update stipend parameters to ₱30,000 (MS) and 
₱38,000 (PhD). 
●​ Umbrella Redundancy: ID 3 functions as a generic master record covering four 
individual programs. If an applicant searches specifically for ERDT (engineering) or 
CBPSME (education), matching queries must route to the specific child records (IDs 
133–136) rather than returning duplicate umbrella entries. 
14. Verification Summary 
●​ Verification Status: Verified 
●​ Last Verified Date: 2026-08-05 
●​ Official URLs Checked: https://www.science-scholarships.ph/, 
https://www.sei.dost.gov.ph/, https://science.upd.edu.ph/dost-asthrdp/ 
●​ Broken Links: None 
●​ Overall Confidence Score: 98/100 
Accelerated Science and Technology Human 
Resource Development Program (ASTHRDP) (ID: 133) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Accelerated Science and Technology Human Resource Development 
Program (ASTHRDP) 
●​ Provider: Department of Science and Technology - Science Education Institute 
(DOST-SEI) 
●​ Category: Government / National / Graduate / Merit-based 
●​ Official Website: https://asthrdp.science-scholarships.ph/ 
●​ Application Portal: https://asthrdp.science-scholarships.ph/ 
●​ Official Guidelines: DOST-SEI ASTHRDP-NSC Information Sheet & Scholar Handbook 
●​ Current Status: Active 
2. Purpose 
To accelerate the production of high-level human resources for Research and Development 
(R&D) in basic and applied S&T fields, improve national global competitiveness, and support 
advanced scientific research in priority areas such as materials science, biotechnology, climate 
change, and drug development. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 


--- PAGE 9 ---
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen. 
●​ Residency: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Education Level: Graduate (Master of Science / PhD in S&T). 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: MS Track: Years 1 and 2; PhD Track: Years 1, 2, and 3 (up to 4 
years for straight-PhD). 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No (Lateral entry graduate students with earned units are 
eligible). 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible for undergraduate degree; must possess a 
completed Bachelor's degree (for MS) or Master's degree (for PhD). 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes (Incoming 1st year graduate students and regular ongoing 
MS/PhD students). 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must be admitted or enrolled in an approved MS/PhD 
program at a National Science Consortium (NSC) member university. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Must meet the graduate school admission standards of the host 
NSC member university. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Governed by host NSC 
university graduate school retention policies). 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Uncapped / No income 
ceiling). 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to ASTHRDP National Science Consortium (NSC) 
Member Universities: UP Diliman, UP Los Baños, UP Manila, UP Visayas, Ateneo de 
Manila University, De La Salle University, University of San Carlos, Mindanao State 
University - Iligan Institute of Technology, Central Luzon State University, and Visayas 
State University. 
●​ Priority Courses: Basic and Applied Sciences: Chemistry, Physics, Biological Sciences, 
Environmental Science, Meteorology/Earth Sciences, Mathematics, Computer Science, 
Materials Science, Food Science, Agriculture, and Biotechnology. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Good Moral: Required (Validated via valid NBI Clearance). 
●​ Health Requirements: Physically and mentally fit to undergo graduate studies, as certified 
by a licensed physician. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must pursue full-time graduate studies; must NOT be employed or 
practicing a profession while on scholarship unless an official approved leave of absence 
(LOA) is granted; must execute a scholarship agreement. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Graduating college seniors entering graduate school, BS graduates 
applying for MS, MS graduates applying for PhD, and ongoing MS/PhD students at NSC 
universities. - Can current freshmen apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate freshmen; 
incoming 1st-year graduate students may apply. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate sophomores; ongoing 
2nd-year MS/PhD students may apply. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate juniors. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Graduating undergraduate seniors entering an MS program 
may apply. 


--- PAGE 10 ---
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes (BS/BA graduates for MS; MS graduates for PhD). 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes. 
5. Application Window 
- Opening Date: Set annually per semester by NSC member universities (e.g., April/May for 1st 
Semester; September/October for 2nd Semester). 
●​ Closing Date: Varies by NSC university (e.g., July 31 for CLSU; August 15 for DLSU). 
●​ Application Cycle: Semester / Term intake. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–2027. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Full actual tuition and other school fees. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: ₱30,000.00 per month for MS scholars; ₱38,000.00 per month for PhD 
scholars. 
●​ Allowance: Learning Materials and/or Connectivity Allowance of ₱20,000.00 per 
Academic Year. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into Learning Materials and Connectivity Allowance 
(₱20,000.00/AY). 
●​ Transportation: Actual one (1) economy class roundtrip fare per Academic Year (for 
scholars studying outside their home province). 
●​ Insurance: Full Group Personal Accident Insurance coverage. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Research Allowance: Student Research Support Fund (SRSF): Outright 
Thesis/Dissertation Allowance of ₱60,000.00 (MS) or ₱100,000.00 (PhD); Research 
Grant up to ₱114,000.00 (MS) or ₱253,000.00 (PhD); Dissemination Grant up to 
₱75,000.00 (MS) or ₱150,000.00 (PhD); Mentor's Fee of ₱36,000.00 (MS) or ₱72,000.00 
(PhD). 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (10% remaining thesis 
grant released upon hardbound submission). 
●​ Return Service: Mandatory service obligation in the Philippines on a full-time basis 
immediately after degree completion, equivalent to one (1) year for every year of 
scholarship enjoyed. 
●​ Other Benefits: Career Incentive Program (CIP) deployment as Graduate Fellow 
post-graduation; MECO-TECO Sandwich Program research exchange eligibility. 
7. Required Documents 
1. Accomplished ASTHRDP-NSC Information Sheet / Application Form. 2. Birth Certificate (PSA 
Photocopy). 3. Certified True Copy of Official Transcript of Records (TOR). 4. Endorsement from 
two (2) former college professors (for MS) or two (2) former MS professors (for PhD). 5. Medical 
Certificate from a licensed physician stating applicant is fit to study. 6. Valid NBI Clearance. 7. 
Official Letter of Admission with regular status from the accepting NSC university. 8. Approved 
Program of Study. 9. If employed: Recommendation and approved LOA from head of agency, or 
proof of resignation. 


--- PAGE 11 ---
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Maintain required cumulative GPA/GWA set by the host NSC university. 
●​ Regular Load: Full-time academic load (9–12 units per regular semester, 3–6 units 
midyear). 
●​ No Failures: No failing or dropped grades in graduate coursework. 
●​ Return Service: Compliance with post-graduation return service contract. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Unapproved employment or practice of profession while holding the scholarship. 
●​ Academic failure or dropping below the host university's required retention GPA. 
●​ Concurrent enjoyment of another government or private scholarship. 
●​ Non-compliance with post-graduation service obligation (requires full financial refund with 
interest).

Engineering Research and Development for 
Technology (ERDT) (ID: 134) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Engineering Research and Development for Technology (ERDT) 
Scholarship Program 
●​ Provider: Department of Science and Technology - Science Education Institute 
(DOST-SEI) 
●​ Category: Government / National / Graduate / Merit-based 
●​ Official Website: https://erdt.science-scholarships.ph/ 
●​ Application Portal: https://erdt.science-scholarships.ph/ 
●​ Official Guidelines: ERDT Graduate Scholarship Program Guidelines & Brochure 
●​ Current Status: Active 
2. Purpose 
To boost high-level engineering human resource capacity in the Philippines, attain a critical 
mass of MS and PhD graduates in engineering, upgrade engineering research capability across 
academia and industry, and foster technological innovation for national industrial development. 


--- PAGE 14 ---
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen. 
●​ Residency: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Education Level: Graduate (Master of Science in Engineering / PhD in Engineering). 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: MS Track: Years 1 and 2; PhD Track: Years 1, 2, and 3 (up to 4 
years for straight-PhD). 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No (Lateral entry ongoing graduate engineering students are 
eligible). 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible for undergraduate degree; must possess a 
completed Bachelor's degree in engineering or related field. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes (Incoming 1st year graduate engineering students and regular 
ongoing MS/PhD engineering scholars). 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must be admitted or enrolled in an approved graduate 
engineering program at an ERDT Consortium member university. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Must satisfy the graduate school admission standards of the 
host ERDT consortium institution. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Governed by host ERDT 
graduate school retention policies). 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Uncapped / No income 
ceiling). 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to the eight (8) ERDT Consortium Member 
Universities: University of the Philippines Diliman, University of the Philippines Los Baños, 
Ateneo de Manila University, De La Salle University, Mapúa University, University of San 
Carlos, Central Luzon State University, and Mindanao State University - Iligan Institute of 
Technology. 
●​ Priority Courses: Graduate Engineering Disciplines: Agricultural and Biosystems 
Engineering, Chemical Engineering, Civil Engineering, Computer Engineering, Electrical 
Engineering, Electronics Engineering, Energy Engineering, Environmental Engineering, 
Industrial Engineering, Materials Science & Engineering, Mechanical Engineering, 
Mining/Metallurgical Engineering. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Good Moral: Required (Validated via valid NBI Clearance). 
●​ Health Requirements: Physically and mentally fit to study, as certified by a licensed 
physician. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must pursue full-time graduate engineering studies; must NOT be 
employed or practicing a profession while on scholarship unless an official approved leave 
of absence (LOA) is granted; must execute a scholarship agreement. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Graduating engineering seniors entering graduate school, BS 
engineering graduates applying for MS, MS engineering graduates applying for PhD, and 
ongoing graduate engineering students at ERDT universities. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate freshmen; incoming 1st-year 


--- PAGE 15 ---
graduate engineering students may apply. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate sophomores; ongoing 
2nd-year MS/PhD engineering students may apply. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate juniors. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Graduating engineering seniors entering an MS program 
upon graduation may apply. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes (BS Engineering graduates for MS; MS Engineering 
graduates for PhD). 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Set per term/semester by ERDT member universities (typically March/April 
for 1st Semester; September/October for 2nd Semester). 
●​ Closing Date: Varies by ERDT university. 
●​ Application Cycle: Semester / Term intake. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–2027. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Full actual tuition and other school fees. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: ₱30,000.00 per month for MS scholars; ₱38,000.00 per month for PhD 
scholars. 
●​ Allowance: Learning Materials and/or Connectivity Allowance of ₱20,000.00 per 
Academic Year. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into Learning Materials and Connectivity Allowance 
(₱20,000.00/AY). 
●​ Transportation: Actual one (1) economy class roundtrip fare per Academic Year (for 
scholars studying outside their home province). 
●​ Insurance: Full Group Personal Accident Insurance coverage. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Research Allowance: Student Research Support Fund (SRSF): Outright 
Thesis/Dissertation Allowance of ₱60,000.00 (MS) or ₱100,000.00 (PhD); Research 
Grant up to ₱114,000.00 (MS) or ₱253,000.00 (PhD); Dissemination Grant up to 
₱75,000.00 (MS) or ₱150,000.00 (PhD); Mentor's Fee of ₱36,000.00 (MS) or ₱72,000.00 
(PhD). 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Return Service: Mandatory service obligation in the Philippines on a full-time basis 
immediately after degree completion, equivalent to one (1) year for every year of 
scholarship enjoyed. 
●​ Other Benefits: Career Incentive Program (CIP) deployment as Graduate Fellow 
post-graduation; MECO-TECO Sandwich Program research exchange eligibility. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Accomplished ERDT Application Form. 
2.​ Birth Certificate (PSA Photocopy). 
3.​ Certified True Copy of Official Transcript of Records (TOR) in Engineering. 


--- PAGE 16 ---
4.​ Endorsement from two (2) former engineering professors. 
5.​ Medical Certificate from a licensed physician. 
6.​ Valid NBI Clearance. 
7.​ Official Letter of Admission with regular status from the accepting ERDT graduate school. 
8.​ Approved Program of Study. 
9.​ If employed: Recommendation and approved LOA from head of agency, or proof of 
resignation. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Maintain required cumulative GPA/GWA set by the host ERDT university. 
●​ Regular Load: Full-time academic load per semester as prescribed in the approved POS. 
●​ No Failures: No failing or dropped grades in graduate engineering coursework. 
●​ Return Service: Compliance with post-graduation return service contract. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Unapproved employment or engineering practice while on scholarship. 
●​ Academic failure or dropping below the ERDT institution's retention GWA. 
●​ Concurrent enjoyment of another government or private scholarship grant. 
●​ Non-compliance with return service obligation (requires full financial refund with interest).

1st Year MS | Eligible Now | Submit employer endorsement and approved LOA. | | Ongoing MS

PhD funding. |

Capacity Building Program in Science and 
Mathematics Education (CBPSME) (ID: 135) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Capacity Building Program in Science and Mathematics Education 
(CBPSME) 
●​ Provider: Department of Science and Technology - Science Education Institute 
(DOST-SEI) 
●​ Category: Government / National / Graduate / Educator Sectoral 
●​ Official Website: https://cbpsme.science-scholarships.ph/ 
●​ Application Portal: https://cbpsme.science-scholarships.ph/ 
●​ Official Guidelines: CBPSME Graduate Scholarship Guidelines & Application Forms 
●​ Current Status: Active 
2. Purpose 
To improve the quality of science and mathematics education in the Philippines by upgrading 
the academic qualifications, professional competence, and research capability of science and 
math teachers and educators across basic and higher education. 


--- PAGE 19 ---
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen. 
●​ Residency: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Education Level: Graduate (Master's / PhD in Science/Mathematics Education). 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: MS Track: Years 1 and 2; PhD Track: Years 1, 2, and 3. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No (Lateral entry ongoing graduate education students are 
eligible). 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible for undergraduate degree; must possess a 
completed Bachelor's degree in education, science, or mathematics. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes (Incoming 1st year graduate education students and regular 
ongoing MS/PhD education scholars). 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must be admitted or enrolled in an approved graduate 
program in Science or Mathematics Education at a CBPSME Consortium University. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Must satisfy the graduate school admission standards of the 
host CBPSME member university. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to CBPSME Consortium Member Universities (e.g., 
Philippine Normal University, DLSU, UP Diliman, West Visayas State University, Bicol 
University, MSU-IIT, University of San Carlos, etc.). 
●​ Priority Courses: Master's and Doctoral Programs in Science Education, Mathematics 
Education, Physics Teaching, Chemistry Teaching, Biology Teaching, and General 
Science Education. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Must be an active Science or Mathematics teacher/educator in 
basic education (DepEd or private school) or a higher education institution. 
●​ Good Moral: Required (Validated via NBI Clearance or Good Moral Certificate). 
●​ Health Requirements: Physically and mentally fit to undergo graduate studies, as certified 
by a licensed physician. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must secure an official study leave of absence from DepEd or 
employer; must execute a service contract binding the scholar to teach or serve in 
science/math education in the Philippines post-graduation. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Science and Mathematics teachers in DepEd/private schools, college 
science/math educators, and qualified graduates entering science/math education 
graduate programs. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate freshmen; incoming 1st-year 
graduate education students may apply. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate sophomores; ongoing 
2nd-year MS/PhD education students may apply. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate juniors. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Graduating education/science majors entering graduate 
studies may apply. 


--- PAGE 20 ---
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes (BS Education/Science graduates for MS; MS Science 
Education graduates for PhD). 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Set annually per semester by CBPSME consortium universities (e.g., 
April/May for 1st Semester; September/October for 2nd Semester). 
●​ Closing Date: Varies by CBPSME university (e.g., June 30 for DepEd endorsement 
deadlines). 
●​ Application Cycle: Semester / Term intake. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–2027. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Full actual tuition and other school fees. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: ₱30,000.00 per month for MS scholars; ₱38,000.00 per month for PhD 
scholars. 
●​ Allowance: Learning Materials and/or Connectivity Allowance of ₱20,000.00 per 
Academic Year. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into Learning Materials and Connectivity Allowance 
(₱20,000.00/AY). 
●​ Transportation: Actual one (1) economy class roundtrip fare per Academic Year (for 
scholars studying outside their home province). 
●​ Insurance: Full Group Personal Accident Insurance coverage. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Research Allowance: Student Research Support Fund (SRSF): Outright 
Thesis/Dissertation Allowance of ₱60,000.00 (MS) or ₱100,000.00 (PhD); Research 
Grant up to ₱114,000.00 (MS) or ₱253,000.00 (PhD); Dissemination Grant up to 
₱75,000.00 (MS) or ₱150,000.00 (PhD); Mentor's Fee of ₱36,000.00 (MS) or ₱72,000.00 
(PhD). 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Return Service: Mandatory service obligation in teaching science/math in the Philippines 
immediately after degree completion, equivalent to one (1) year for every year of 
scholarship enjoyed. 
●​ Other Benefits: Special Science Teacher hiring priority under RA 10612 mechanism for 
qualified scholar-graduates; CIP deployment eligibility. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Accomplished CBPSME Application Form. 
2.​ Birth Certificate (PSA Photocopy). 
3.​ Certified True Copy of Official Transcript of Records (TOR). 
4.​ Endorsement from two (2) former professors or school administrators. 
5.​ Medical Certificate from a licensed physician. 
6.​ Valid NBI Clearance. 
7.​ Letter of Admission with regular status from accepting CBPSME university. 
8.​ Approved Program of Study. 


--- PAGE 21 ---
9.​ Official DepEd or employer recommendation and approved Leave of Absence (LOA). 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Maintain required cumulative GPA/GWA set by host CBPSME university. 
●​ Regular Load: Full-time credit load per semester as prescribed in POS. 
●​ No Failures: No failing grades in graduate education subjects. 
●​ Return Service: Compliance with post-graduation teaching return service contract. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Failure to secure an approved official leave of absence from DepEd or employer. 
●​ Academic failure or dropping below host university's retention standards. 
●​ Concurrent enjoyment of another government scholarship. 
●​ Refusal to render mandatory science/math teaching return service post-graduation.

Science and Technology Education for Regional 
Development (STRAND) (ID: 136) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Science and Technology Education for Regional Development (STRAND) - 
(formerly Science and Technology Regional Alliance of Universities for National 
Development) 
●​ Provider: Department of Science and Technology - Science Education Institute 
(DOST-SEI) 
●​ Category: Government / National / Graduate / Institutional Faculty Sectoral 
●​ Official Website: https://www.science-scholarships.ph/ 
●​ Application Portal: https://www.science-scholarships.ph/ 
●​ Official Guidelines: DOST-SEI STRAND Graduate Scholarship Guidelines 
●​ Current Status: Active 
2. Purpose 
To strengthen the academic and research capability of regional higher education institutions 
(HEIs) outside the National Capital Region (NCR) by providing graduate scholarships to 
institutional faculty members, enhancing regional S&T human resource development. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen. 
●​ Residency: Regional / Provincial HEI employment focus. 
●​ Education Level: Graduate (Master's Degree and PhD in S&T). 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: MS Track: Years 1 and 2; PhD Track: Years 1, 2, and 3. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No (Lateral entry ongoing graduate students are eligible). 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible for undergraduate degree; must possess a 
completed Bachelor's degree (for MS) or Master's degree (for PhD). 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes (Incoming 1st year graduate students and regular ongoing 
MS/PhD faculty scholars). 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must be admitted or enrolled in an approved graduate 
S&T program at a STRAND delivering/sending consortium university. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Must meet the graduate admission standards of the accepting 
STRAND university. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted to STRAND Member Delivering and Sending Higher 
Education Institutions (Provincial/Regional SUCs and HEIs outside NCR). 
●​ Priority Courses: Priority S&T Graduate Programs aligned with regional development 
agendas. 


--- PAGE 24 ---
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Must be a regular or plantilla faculty member of an eligible 
regional/provincial higher education institution (HEI). 
●​ Good Moral: Required (Validated via NBI Clearance or Good Moral Certificate). 
●​ Health Requirements: Physically and mentally fit to undergo graduate studies, as certified 
by a licensed physician. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must secure an official study leave of absence (LOA) from the home 
sending HEI; must execute a service contract to return to the home regional HEI to render 
service upon degree completion. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Regular/plantilla faculty members of regional SUCs and HEIs pursuing 
MS or PhD degrees in priority S&T fields. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate freshmen; incoming 1st-year 
graduate faculty scholars may apply. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate sophomores; ongoing 
2nd-year MS/PhD faculty scholars may apply. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Ineligible for undergraduate juniors. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Ineligible (must be an employed faculty member of a regional 
HEI). 
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes (Faculty members holding BS degrees applying for MS, or 
holding MS applying for PhD). 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Set annually per semester by STRAND consortium universities. 
●​ Closing Date: Varies by regional consortium university. 
●​ Application Cycle: Semester / Term intake. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–2027. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Full actual tuition and other school fees. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: ₱30,000.00 per month for MS scholars; ₱38,000.00 per month for PhD 
scholars. 
●​ Allowance: Learning Materials and/or Connectivity Allowance of ₱20,000.00 per 
Academic Year. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into Learning Materials and Connectivity Allowance 
(₱20,000.00/AY). 
●​ Transportation: Actual one (1) economy class roundtrip fare per Academic Year (for 
scholars studying outside their home province). 
●​ Insurance: Full Group Personal Accident Insurance coverage. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Research Allowance: Student Research Support Fund (SRSF): Outright 
Thesis/Dissertation Allowance of ₱60,000.00 (MS) or ₱100,000.00 (PhD); Research 
Grant up to ₱114,000.00 (MS) or ₱253,000.00 (PhD); Dissemination Grant up to 
₱75,000.00 (MS) or ₱150,000.00 (PhD); Mentor's Fee of ₱36,000.00 (MS) or ₱72,000.00 


--- PAGE 25 ---
(PhD). 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Return Service: Mandatory return service obligation to the sending regional HEI 
immediately after degree completion, equivalent to one (1) year for every year of 
scholarship enjoyed. 
●​ Other Benefits: Career Incentive Program (CIP) deployment eligibility as Graduate Fellow; 
MECO-TECO Sandwich Program research exchange eligibility. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Accomplished STRAND Application Form. 
2.​ Birth Certificate (PSA Photocopy). 
3.​ Certified True Copy of Official Transcript of Records (TOR). 
4.​ Endorsement from two (2) former professors or department heads. 
5.​ Medical Certificate from a licensed physician.

8.​ Approved Program of Study.

of the sending regional HEI. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Maintain required cumulative GPA/GWA set by host STRAND university. 
●​ Regular Load: Full-time credit load per term as prescribed in POS. 
●​ No Failures: No failing grades in graduate coursework. 
●​ Return Service: Fulfill post-graduation return service to home regional university. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Non-faculty status or failure to secure an approved LOA from the regional sending HEI. 
●​ Academic failure or dropping below host university retention standards. 
●​ Concurrent enjoyment of another government scholarship. 
●​ Failure to return to the home regional HEI to render service post-graduation.

Systemic Database Audit & Production Migration 
Architecture 
The canonical auditing of DOST-SEI graduate scholarship records (IDs 3, 133, 134, 135, and 
136) demonstrates significant structural divergence between live database parameters and 
governing agency mandates. Master umbrella record ID 3 suffers from parameter corruption, 
including an erroneous ₱500,000 income limit and an understated ₱15,000 monthly living 
allowance. Meanwhile, child records IDs 133–136 contain incomplete schema mappings and 
lack domain-specific sectoral tags. 
To align the production database (public.scholarships) with canonical Tier 1 requirements, 
database administrators must execute structural schema extensions and data mutation scripts. 
Schema Alteration Script (DDL) 
To support explicit temporal, institutional, and sectoral filtering without relying on loose string 
matching, the live SQL table must be extended: 
-- Extend 
public.sc[span_526](start_span)[span_526](end_span)[span_532](start_sp
an)[span_532](end_span)holarships with strict structural constraints​
ALTER TABLE public.scholarships​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS eligible_year_levels INT[] DEFAULT '{1}',​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS incoming_year_only BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS requires_current_enrollment BOOLEAN DEFAULT 
false,​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS renewal_gwa NUMERIC(5,2) DEFAULT NULL,​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS parent_employment_restriction VARCHAR(100) 
DEFAULT NULL,​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS sectoral_restriction VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 
NULL,​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS degree_program_restricted TEXT[] DEFAULT 
NULL,​

false;​

Multi-Stage Matching Engine Pipeline Architecture 
To eliminate matching errors, the live recommendation engine processes user vectors through a 
three-stage filtering pipeline: 
1.​ Stage 1: Hard Temporal and Academic Level Filtering 
○​ The engine verifies that user.education_level == 'Graduate' and user.earned_units 
>= 0. 
○​ It validates that user.year_level is contained within scholarship.eligible_year_levels. 
○​ It evaluates user.is_[span_25](start_span)[span_25](end_span)currently_enrolled 
against scholarship.requires_current_enrollment. 
2.​ Stage 2: Socio-Economic and Employment Validation 
○​ The pipeline checks employment status (user.is_employed): if the applicant is 
employed, it verifies that user.has_approved_loa == true. 
○​ It bypasses income ceiling checks for DOST graduate programs where 
income_limit is NULL. 
3.​ Stage 3: Institutional, Sectoral, and Program Alignment 
○​ The engine matches user.school_id against specific consortium member university 
tables (such as asthrdp_nsc_schools, erdt_consortium_schools, 
cbpsme_consortium_schools, or strand_consortium_schools). 
○​ It validates user.sectoral_status by checking teacher credentials for CBPSME (ID 
135) and regional HEI faculty status for STRAND (ID 136). 
○​ It confirms user.course_code against approved priority S&T graduate fields. 


--- PAGE 31 ---
Executing these database updates and implementing the multi-stage matching pipeline aligns 
the ISKONNECT database with canonical governing policies, ensuring reliable scholarship 
recommendations for Filipino graduate students and educators.

ISKONNECT Production Verification 
Audit: Group C Foreign & International 
Scholarships 
This canonical verification report provides an exhaustive, production-grade audit of eleven 
international and bilateral scholarship programs available to Filipino applicants1. Developed for 
the automated scholarship matching platform ISKONNECT, this report establishes the 
immutable source of truth required for production database mutations and matching engine 
rule configurations1. 
Every parameter has been strictly validated against Tier 1 primary sources, including official 
government portals, governing diplomatic announcements, downloadable application 
guidebooks, and official program memoranda1. In accordance with platform audit protocols, 
any parameter that is not explicitly mandated in governing primary documentation is 
designated strictly as NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE to prevent matching engine 
distortion1. 
Taiwan ICDF Higher Education Scholarship Program 
(ID: 60) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: TaiwanICDF International Higher Education Scholarship Program3 
●​ Provider: Taiwan International Cooperation and Development Fund (TaiwanICDF)3 
●​ Category: Foreign Government / International / Merit-based / Graduate3 
●​ Official Website: https://www.icdf.org.tw3 
●​ Application Portal: https://www.icdf.org.tw / TaiwanICDF Online Application System3 
●​ Official Guidelines: TaiwanICDF International Higher Education Scholarship Program 
Application Guidebook4 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To assist friendly and developing partner nations in human resource development by providing 
full financial support for talented international students to pursue Master's and Doctoral 
degrees across designated partner universities in Taiwan5. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Citizen of an eligible partner country, including the Philippines4. 
●​ Residency: Resident in the country of citizenship4. 


--- PAGE 2 ---
●​ Education Level: Graduate (Master's and Doctoral degree levels)3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Year 1 (Incoming Graduate Students)4. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No4. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible for initial award unless applying for entry-level 
graduate degree studies4. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes3. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must apply for admission to a designated TaiwanICDF 
partner university program4. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Outstanding academic record from prior post-secondary 
studies4. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Evaluated holistically via 
transcripts and university admission standards; live database parameter lists 85.00%)3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Age Restrictions: Must be above 18 years of age4. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to designated TaiwanICDF partner institutions4. 
●​ Priority Courses: Agriculture, Science and Engineering, Public Health and Medicine, 
Business Administration7. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Good Moral: Good moral character required4. 
●​ Health Requirements: Satisfactory physical and mental health4. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Applicants cannot hold any other Republic of China (Taiwan) 
government scholarship concurrently8. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Bachelor's degree holders applying for Master's programs, and Master's 
degree holders applying for PhD programs at partner Taiwanese universities4. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: No 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: No 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: No 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes (provided they graduate prior to scholarship intake)8. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes4. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes4. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Mid-January / February 1 annually8. 
●​ Closing Date: March 15 / March 31 annually4. 
●​ Application Cycle: Annual3. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2026–20274. 


--- PAGE 3 ---
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: 100% full coverage of tuition and credit fees3. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: NTD 15,000 for Master's students; NTD 17,000–18,000 for PhD 
students4. 
●​ Allowance: Campus housing / dormitory allowance provided4. 
●​ Book Allowance: Full textbook costs covered4. 
●​ Transportation: Economy class round-trip airfare4. 
●​ Insurance: Full health and accident insurance coverage4. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Research Allowance: Integrated into graduate institute research allocations4. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Return Service: None required by TaiwanICDF, though scholars are expected to return 
home to foster local development3. 
●​ Other Benefits: Pre-degree Mandarin language orientation8. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Completed Online Application Form4. 
2.​ Passport Biopage / Certificate of Nationality4. 
3.​ Highest Degree Diploma and Official Academic Transcripts4. 
4.​ Proof of English Language Proficiency (TOEFL / IELTS / Official Institutional Certificate)4. 
5.​ Two Letters of Recommendation4. 
6.​ Copy of Application Submission to a TaiwanICDF Partner University4. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Satisfy academic GPA retention thresholds established by the host 
institution4. 
●​ Regular Load: Full-time credit load per semester4. 
●​ No Failures: Zero failing grades in enrolled coursework4. 
●​ Return Service: None3. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Holding ROC (Taiwan) citizenship or overseas Chinese student status4. 
●​ Concurrent enjoyment of another Taiwanese government scholarship8. 
●​ Failure to secure official admission from an approved TaiwanICDF partner university 
program4.

Erasmus Mundus Joint Masters Scholarship (ID: 63) 


--- PAGE 6 ---
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Erasmus Mundus Joint Masters Scholarship3 
●​ Provider: European Union / European Education and Culture Executive Agency (EACEA)3 
●​ Category: Foreign Government / International / Merit-based / Graduate3 
●​ Official Website: https://erasmus-plus.ec.europa.eu3 
●​ Application Portal: 
https://www.eacea.ec.europa.eu/scholarships/erasmus-mundus-catalogue_en9 
●​ Official Guidelines: Erasmus+ Programme Guide (Key Action 2: Erasmus Mundus 
Action)11 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To fund high-level, integrated transnational study programs at the Master level delivered by 
international university consortia, fostering academic excellence, international cooperation, and 
global student mobility across Europe and beyond9. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Open to candidates worldwide, including Filipino citizens10. 
●​ Residency: Worldwide residency; mandatory physical mobility across at least two 
different host countries11. 
●​ Education Level: Graduate (Master's degree level, 60, 90, or 120 ECTS)10. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Incoming Master's students10. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No10. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Graduating Bachelor's students eligible provided their degree 
is conferred prior to intake10. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes10. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must hold a recognized first higher education degree 
(Bachelor's degree or equivalent)10. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Outstanding academic performance in prior undergraduate 
studies10. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Consortium-specific; live DB list is 
90.00%)3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (No upper age limit). 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted to participating Erasmus Mundus Joint Master consortia 
HEIs9. 
●​ Priority Courses: Comprehensive academic fields listed in the Erasmus Mundus 
Catalogue9.

●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Good Moral: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Health Requirements: Health insurance covered under EU framework10. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Mandatory physical mobility in at least two different countries11; 
maximum application limit of three (3) EMJM programs per application cycle9. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Bachelor's degree holders or final-year undergraduate students 
graduating prior to program start10. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: No 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: No 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: No 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes (if graduating before Master's intake)10. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes10. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes9.

●​ Closing Date: January / February 12 (varies per consortium)9.

●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2026–202713. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: 100% full coverage of participation costs, tuition, and enrollment fees10. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: €1,400 per month living allowance (up to 24 months maximum)3. 
●​ Allowance: Travel, visa, and installation contributions integrated into overall grant10. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into monthly living stipend10. 
●​ Transportation: Travel cost allowance included10. 
●​ Insurance: Comprehensive health and accident insurance coverage10. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Research Allowance: Research and thesis preparation costs included10. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Return Service: None3. 
●​ Other Benefits: Award of a joint degree or multiple degrees upon completion10. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Bachelor's Diploma or Official Certificate of Expected Graduation10. 
2.​ Official Academic Transcripts of Records (TOR)10. 
3.​ Proof of English Language Proficiency (IELTS / TOEFL)9. 
4.​ Motivation Letter / Statement of Purpose9.

5.​ Two Academic / Professional Recommendation Letters9. 
6.​ Passport / Proof of Nationality9.

regulations11.

●​ No Failures: Pass all mandatory study modules11. 
●​ Return Service: None3. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Failure to complete Bachelor's degree prior to Master's program commencement10.

,

16.

Fulbright-PhilSA Graduate Program – PAEF / PhilSA 
(ID: 90) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Fulbright-Philippine Space Agency (PhilSA) Foreign Student Program in 
Space Science and Technology Applications (SSTA)30 
●​ Provider: Philippine-American Educational Foundation (PAEF / Fulbright Commission) & 
Philippine Space Agency (PhilSA)30 
●​ Category: Foreign Government / Joint Agency / Merit-based / Graduate3 
●​ Official Website: https://www.fulbright.org.ph / https://philsa.gov.ph3 
●​ Application Portal: https://apply.iie.org/ffsp20273 
●​ Official Guidelines: Fulbright-PhilSA Foreign Student Program Call for Applications30 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To accelerate the development of a sustainable pool of space scientists, engineers, lawyers, 
entrepreneurs, and communicators by providing full funding for graduate studies (Master's and 
PhD) in the United States30. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino Citizen residing in the Philippines at application and selection time; 
dual citizens or US permanent residents are barred30. 


--- PAGE 42 ---
●​ Residency: Resident in the Philippines30. 
●​ Education Level: Graduate (Master's or Doctoral studies)3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Year 1 (Incoming Graduate Students)30. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No30. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible30. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes30. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Completed Bachelor's degree with major in field of 
specialization and an excellent academic record31. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Excellent undergraduate academic record31. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Evaluated via transcript and 
research objective statement)3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ School Restrictions: Accredited Higher Education Institutions in the United States30. 
●​ Priority Courses: Space Applications (Earth observation, meteorology, PNT, telecom), 
Spacecraft Systems (satellites, rocketry, robotics, cybersecurity), Space Science 
(astronomy, space biology/medicine), Space Law, Economics, and Administration30. 
●​ Work Experience: Minimum two (2) years of professional work experience after college 
graduation30. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Space Science and Technology Applications (SSTA) and allied 
sectors31. 
●​ Good Moral: No pending administrative or criminal charges; no conviction30. 
●​ Health Requirements: Physically and mentally fit to pursue graduate studies in the US30. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must commit to returning to the Philippines immediately upon 
program completion to fulfill return service30; no dependent support provided31. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Bachelor's degree holders with at least 2 years post-college 
professional work experience in space-related disciplines30. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: No 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: No 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: No 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: No 
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes (Bachelor's or Master's degree graduates)30. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes (if not received a Fulbright grant within the past 5 
years)30. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: January 20 annually31. 


--- PAGE 43 ---
●​ Closing Date: April 18 / April 30 / June 19 (depending on AY cycle announcement)30. 
●​ Application Cycle: Annual3. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2026–2027 / AY 2027–202830. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: 100% full coverage of tuition and university fees3. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: Monthly maintenance allowance30. 
●​ Allowance: Settling-in allowance, in-transit allowance, allowable excess baggage grant31. 
●​ Book Allowance: Books and supplies allowance30. 
●​ Transportation: International round-trip airfare30. 
●​ Insurance: Health and accident insurance30. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Research Allowance: Research objective support included30. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Return Service: Mandatory Return Service obligation in the Philippines immediately upon 
program completion3. 
●​ Other Benefits: Funding up to 1–2 years for Master's, 2 years for Doctoral studies30. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Completed Online Application via IIE Portal30. 
2.​ Research Objective Statement (3 to 5 pages)30. 
3.​ Personal Statement (maximum 3 pages)30. 
4.​ Updated Curriculum Vitae / Resume (maximum 6 pages)30. 
5.​ Official Transcripts of Records and Diplomas30. 
6.​ Three Letters of Recommendation30. 
7.​ Passport Biopage Copy30. 
8.​ NBI Clearance (secured within 6 months)30. 
9.​ Writing Samples (maximum 20 pages) & Bibliography30. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Satisfy academic GPA retention policies of host US university30. 
●​ Regular Load: Full credit load30. 
●​ No Failures: Zero failed academic units30. 
●​ Return Service: Immediate return to the Philippines post-graduation30. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Holding dual US citizenship or permanent resident status30. 
●​ Having less than two years post-college professional work experience30. 
●​ Presently living, studying, or working in the United States30.

Chevening Scholarship – FCDO / UK Government (ID: 
91) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Chevening Scholarship3 
●​ Provider: Foreign, Commonwealth & Development Office (FCDO) / UK Government3 
●​ Category: Foreign Government / International / Merit-based / Graduate3 
●​ Official Website: https://www.chevening.org/apply3 
●​ Application Portal: Chevening Online Application System34 
●​ Official Guidelines: Chevening Scholarship Terms and Conditions36 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To enable outstanding emerging leaders from around the world to pursue one-year taught 
Master's degrees at accredited universities across the United Kingdom, building lasting global 
networks and leadership capacity36. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino Citizen (or citizen of a Chevening-eligible territory)34. 
●​ Residency: Resident in the Philippines or an eligible Chevening territory34. 
●​ Education Level: Graduate (One-year taught Master's degree)3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Incoming Master's Students36. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No37. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible37. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes36. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must have completed an undergraduate degree 
enabling entry into a UK postgraduate program10. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Meets UK university Master's entry requirements37. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Must obtain an unconditional offer 
from a UK university; live database parameter lists NULL)3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (No upper age limit). 
●​ School Restrictions: Any recognized UK Higher Education Institution36. 
●​ Priority Courses: Any eligible one-year taught Master's degree program in the UK (MBA 


--- PAGE 47 ---
fee contribution capped at £22,000)36. 
●​ Work Experience: Minimum two (2) years of work experience (equivalent to 2,800 
hours)24. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: High leadership potential / emerging leaders37. 
●​ Good Moral: Good moral character; compliance with Chevening Code of Conduct37. 
●​ Health Requirements: Must receive medical clearance and UK visa entry clearance37. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must return to home country for at least two (2) years after 
scholarship completion36; no financial or visa support provided for dependants37. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Bachelor's degree holders with at least 2 years work experience37. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: No 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: No 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: No 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: No 
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes (Bachelor's degree graduates)37. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes (if not previously funded by a UK government 
grant). 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: August / September annually3. 
●​ Closing Date: November / October 7 annually (e.g., October 7, 2026 in DB)3. 
●​ Application Cycle: Annual3. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2026–20273. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: 100% full tuition fee coverage (MBA fee contribution capped at £22,000)3. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: Personal living allowance (stipend rate varies for London vs 
Non-London institutions)36. 
●​ Allowance: Arrival allowance, departure allowance, travel top-up allowance for London 
events36. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into monthly living allowance37. 
●​ Transportation: Economy class return air travel to/from country of residence36. 
●​ Insurance: Covered under UK visa clearance; scholars advised to secure travel 
insurance37. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Return Service: Mandatory 2-year return to home country following completion of 
award3. 
●​ Other Benefits: UK visa application fee coverage, contribution of up to £75 for TB testing 


--- PAGE 48 ---
where required36. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Completed Online Application Form via Chevening portal34. 
2.​ Official Undergraduate Transcripts and Degree Certificate37. 
3.​ Selection of Three Eligible Taught UK Master's Courses36. 
4.​ Two Reference Letters37. 
5.​ Valid Passport / Proof of Citizenship37. 
6.​ Evidence of 2 Years Work Experience (2,800 hours)24. 
7.​ Unconditional Offer Letter from at least one UK course choice (by July deadline)36. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Satisfy academic progression rules of host UK university37. 
●​ Regular Load: Continuous full-time enrollment37. 
●​ No Failures: Complete all Master's course modules37. 
●​ Return Service: Mandatory return to home country for 2 years36. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Holding British or dual British citizenship37. 
●​ Having less than two years (2,800 hours) work experience24. 
●​ Previous receipt of a UK government-funded scholarship36.

Strategic Recommendations for ISKONNECT 
Architecture 
The integration of these international and bilateral scholarship parameters requires specific 
enhancements to the ISKONNECT multi-stage recommendation pipeline1. 
First, professional work experience validation must be added as a mandatory Stage 2 query 
constraint1. Programs such as Australia Awards (ID 74), Fulbright-PhilSA (ID 90), and Chevening 
(ID 91) strictly require at least two years of post-graduation work experience24. Without 
validating user.work_experience_years >= 2, undergraduate seniors and fresh graduates will 
receive false-positive recommendations, leading to immediate disqualification during agency 
eligibility screening1. 
Second, foreign GPA conversion routines must be standardized within the recommendation 
logic1. Global Korea Scholarship tracks (IDs 64 & 65) mandate a minimum cumulative grade 
point average of 80% or placement in the top 20% of the class19. The engine must normalize 
Philippine General Weighted Average (GWA) values—where 1.0 represents the highest score 
and 3.0 represents passing—into standard NIIED percentile equivalents to prevent 
false-negative omissions of eligible top-tier Filipino students1. 
Third, temporal age filters must be calculated relative to fixed intake benchmark dates rather 
than real-time application submission dates1. MEXT tracks (IDs 81, 82, 83) set strict age cutoffs 
based on birthdates on or after April 2, 200229. Similarly, GKS Undergraduate (ID 65) requires 
candidates to be under 25 years of age as of the intake year18. Evaluating user birthdates 
dynamically against these intake thresholds ensures exact temporal eligibility alignment across 
the platform1.

Canonical Verification Report for 
ISKONNECT Production Database: Local 
Government Unit & Institutional 
Scholarship Tracks 
Taguig LANI Premier Scholarship (ID: 27) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Taguig City Lifeline Assistance for Neighbors In-Need (L.A.N.I.) Premier 
Scholarship1 
●​ Provider: Taguig City Government / Taguig Scholarship Secretariat1 
●​ Category: Local Government Unit (LGU) / Merit-based1 
●​ Official Website: https://www.taguig.gov.ph1 
●​ Application Portal: https://tcu.edu.ph/lani-scholarship1 
●​ Official Guidelines: Taguig City Ordinance No. 9, Series of 2011; Executive Order No. 
2011-111 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To provide financial assistance and merit incentives to qualified Taguig City residents pursuing 
higher education in top-tier higher education institutions, specifically the University of the 
Philippines System (Luzon campuses) and private/public higher education institutions in the 
National Capital Region (NCR) recognized as Centers of Excellence by the Commission on 
Higher Education (CHED)1. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino Citizen1 
●​ Residency: Bona fide resident of Taguig City for at least three (3) years immediately 
preceding the application1. 
●​ Education Level: College / Undergraduate1. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1, 2, 3, 4, 51. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No1. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes1. 
●​ Graduate Students?: No (Covered under LEAD track ID 97)1. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Enrolled or enrolling in the UP System (Luzon 


--- PAGE 2 ---
campuses) or CHED-certified Centers of Excellence in NCR1. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Senior High School or college academic performance meeting 
admission and retention cutoffs1. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 90% (or equivalent 1.75 semestral weighted average)1. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted to University of the Philippines System (Luzon campuses) 
and CHED-certified Centers of Excellence in NCR1. 
●​ Priority Courses: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Good Moral: Required (Certificate of Good Moral Character issued for the current 
school year)1. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must be a registered voter of Taguig City if 18 years or older, with at 
least one parent registered as a voter of Taguig City1. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Graduating SHS students and ongoing college students enrolled in 
qualifying COE institutions or UP Luzon campuses1. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes1. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes1. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes1. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes1. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No1. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes (Requires semestral renewal)1. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Semestral schedule announced by the Taguig Scholarship Secretariat1. 
●​ Closing Date: Announced per semestral intake window1. 
●​ Application Cycle: Semestral3. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–20273. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Integrated into financial grant1. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: Integrated into semestral allowance1. 
●​ Allowance: PHP 40,000.00 to PHP 50,000.00 per school year (PHP 20,000.00 to PHP 
25,000.00 per semester)1. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into semestral allowance1. 
●​ Transportation: Integrated into semestral allowance1. 


--- PAGE 3 ---
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Return Service: None mandated; scholars are encouraged to serve Taguig City1. 
●​ Other Benefits: Merit incentives awarded to scholars obtaining a semestral weighted 
average of 1.75 or higher1. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Completely filled-out LANI Scholarship Application Form with recent 2x2 pictures1. 
2.​ Registration Form or Official Receipt of Enrollment for the current semester1. 
3.​ Authenticated Copy of Grades/Transcript of Records for the preceding semester1. 
4.​ Certificate of Good Moral Character issued for the current academic year1. 
5.​ Voter's Certification issued by COMELEC (for applicant if >=18 years old, and parent)1. 
6.​ Certificate of Residency (minimum 3 years)1. 
7.​ Proof of Billing under the applicant's or parent's name1. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Must maintain a General Weighted Average (GWA) not lower than 2.50 
per semester5. 
●​ Regular Load: Enrolled in at least 15 academic units per semester or equivalent per 
trimester5. 
●​ No Failures: Zero failing grades (5.0), unremoved 4.0, Incomplete (INC), or Dropped 
marks5. 
●​ Return Service: None1. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Residing in Taguig City for less than three (3) consecutive years1. 
●​ Non-voter status of applicant (if 18+) or parent in Taguig City1. 
●​ Semester GWA dropping below 2.50 or receiving grades of 5.0, 4.0, INC, or Dropped5. 
●​ Enrollment in non-COE institutions or non-UP Luzon campuses1.

Taguig LANI Priority Scholarship (ID: 28) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Taguig City L.A.N.I. Priority Courses and Skills Training Scholarship1 
●​ Provider: Taguig City Government / Taguig Scholarship Secretariat1 
●​ Category: Local Government Unit (LGU) / Merit-and-Need1 
●​ Official Website: https://www.taguig.gov.ph1 
●​ Application Portal: https://tcu.edu.ph/lani-scholarship1 
●​ Official Guidelines: Taguig City Ordinance No. 9, Series of 2011; Executive Order No. 
2011-111 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To incentivize Taguig City residents to pursue priority science, technology, law, and medical 
degree programs in government-accredited institutions, as well as to support persons with 
disabilities (PWDs) undergoing specialized training1. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino Citizen1 
●​ Residency: Bona fide resident of Taguig City for at least three (3) years immediately 
preceding the application1. 
●​ Education Level: College / Undergraduate / Professional (Law & Medicine)1. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1, 2, 3, 4, 51. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No1. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes1. 
●​ Graduate Students?: No (Except Law and Medicine)1.

●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted to DOST-listed institutions, top PRC/CHED performing 
law/medical schools, or PDAO-accredited institutions1. 
●​ Priority Courses: DOST S&T Priority Courses, Law (Juris Doctor), Medicine (Doctor of 
Medicine)1. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: PWD applicants must submit an official ID/endorsement from the

●​ Good Moral: Required1. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Other Official Rules: Applicant and/or parent must be registered voters of Taguig City1.

1. Scholarship Identity

2. Purpose

qualified Makati public high school graduates enrolled in State Universities and Colleges (SUCs) 
in Metro Manila or DOST priority programs6. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino Citizen6 
●​ Residency: Bona fide resident of Makati City6. 
●​ Education Level: College / Undergraduate3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1 (Incoming Freshmen)6. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: Yes6. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible for initial entry6. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible6. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Enrolled or accepted as an incoming 1st-year student in 
any Metro Manila SUC (other than UMak) or DOST-accredited priority course6. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Fresh senior high school graduate belonging to the Top 10 
Percent of the graduating class6. 


--- PAGE 20 ---
●​ Minimum GWA: Minimum GWA of 1.50 (or equivalent 88–90% scale)3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: Belong to the Top 10% of the SHS graduating class6. 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ School Restrictions: Metro Manila SUCs (excluding UMak) or DOST-accredited schools6. 
●​ Priority Courses: DOST-listed priority courses6. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: PWDs enrolled in top performing CHED/PRC schools qualify 
under the PHP 40,000 track6. 
●​ Good Moral: Required6. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must sign a mandatory Service Agreement committing to serve with 
the Makati City Government upon graduation6. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Graduating SHS students from Makati public schools belonging to the 
top 10% of their class6. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes (at initial college entry)6. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: No6. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: No6. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: No6. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No6. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: No6. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Annual cycle announced following SHS graduation6. 
●​ Closing Date: Specified per annual intake notice6. 
●​ Application Cycle: Fixed / Annual3. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–20273. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Covered per institutional billing6. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: Integrated into total grant6. 
●​ Allowance: SUC Track: PHP 20,000.00 total benefit per school year; DOST Priority 
Courses / PWD Track: PHP 40,000.00 total benefit per school year6. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into grant6. 
●​ Transportation: Integrated into grant6. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 


--- PAGE 21 ---
●​ Return Service: Mandatory service agreement to serve with the Makati City Government 
after graduation6. 
●​ Other Benefits: None6. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Official Application Form6. 
2.​ SHS Form 138 showing GWA and Certification of Top 10% Class Rank signed by 
Principal6. 
3.​ Proof of Residency in Makati City (Barangay Certificate / Voter's ID)6. 
4.​ Certificate of Enrollment / Admission Notice from Metro Manila SUC or DOST-accredited 
HEI6. 
5.​ Parents' Income Tax Return or Certificate of Indigency6. 
6.​ Signed Service Contract Agreement6. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Must maintain a GWA of at least 1.50 (or equivalent) each term6. 
●​ Regular Load: Full academic credit load required6. 
●​ No Failures: No grade of 5.0, 4.0, Incomplete, or Dropped subjects6. 
●​ Return Service: Compliance with post-graduation service contract6. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Failing grades (5.0), unremoved 4.0, or incomplete marks6. 
●​ Refusal to sign or fulfill the mandatory Makati City Government service agreement6. 
●​ Non-residency in Makati City6.

University of Makati Token Fee Exemption Program 


--- PAGE 24 ---
(ID: 32) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: University of Makati (UMak) Token Fee Exemption Program7 
●​ Provider: University of Makati / City Government of Makati7 
●​ Category: Institutional / Local University / Merit-and-Need3 
●​ Official Website: https://www.umak.edu.ph7 
●​ Application Portal: UMak OLEA Portal 
(https://www.umak.edu.ph/admissions/scholarships/)7 
●​ Official Guidelines: UMak City Ordinance No. 2024-108; UMak Revised Scholarship 
Guidelines AY 2025–20267 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To grant full or partial exemption from institutional token and tuition fees to qualified students 
enrolled at the University of Makati based on academic honors, residency, and retention 
performance7. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino Citizen7 
●​ Residency: Priority given to bona fide Makati City residents (determined via UMak 
residency verification)10. Non-Makati residents eligible for specific honor tracks7. 
●​ Education Level: College / Undergraduate3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1, 2, 3, 4, 57. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No (Entrance exemption is freshman-only; academic retention 
applies to all years)7. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes7. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Restricted (Separate graduate fee schedules apply)10. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must be officially admitted and enrolled at the 
University of Makati7. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Entrance exemption requires SHS graduation with "Highest 
Honor" (GWA 98–100) or "High Honor" (GWA 95–97)7. Continuing exemption governed 
by semestral GWA cutoffs8. 
●​ Minimum GWA: Entrance cutoff: 95.00% (High Honor) or 98.00% (Highest Honor)7. 
Renewal cutoff: GWA <= 2.50 (If GWA is 2.75–3.00, student pays PHP 2,000/unit)11. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: Senior High School Honor Roll certification7. 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to students enrolled at the University of Makati7. 


--- PAGE 25 ---
●​ Priority Courses: Any undergraduate degree program offered by UMak7. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Good Moral: Required (Good disciplinary standing)7. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Other Official Rules: Governed by UMak retention policies; unofficially dropped subjects 
cause forfeiture7. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Officially enrolled UMak undergraduate students7. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes7. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes7. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes7. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes7. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No7. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes (Automated evaluation or semestral application on 
OLEA portal)7. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Scheduled per semester on the OLEA system (e.g., January intake for 2nd 
Sem)7. 
●​ Closing Date: Specified per term registration schedule12. 
●​ Application Cycle: Semestral3. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–20273. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: 100% exemption from tuition/token fees for qualified honor entrants and 
high-performing scholars7. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Allowance: Token exemption value equivalent to PHP 1,000.00 – PHP 5,000.00 per 
semester depending on residency tier3. 
●​ Book Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Transportation: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Return Service: None7. 
●​ Other Benefits: Exemption from paying per-unit fee penalties11. 
7. Required Documents 


--- PAGE 26 ---
1.​ SHS Grade 12 Report Card (1st and 2nd Semesters)7. 
2.​ Certificate of Highest Honor (GWA 98–100) or High Honor (GWA 95–97) signed by 
Principal7. 
3.​ Voter's Certification of applicant or parent (for Makati resident tagging)10. 
4.​ Official UMak Grade Report for preceding semester (for continuing applicants)7. 
5.​ Online application submission via UMak OLEA account7. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Must maintain prescribed GWA per semester (GWA <= 2.50)8. 
●​ Regular Load: Full academic load carried each term7. 
●​ No Failures: Zero failing grades or unofficially dropped subjects7. 
●​ Return Service: None7. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ GWA dropping between 2.75 and 3.00 results in loss of exemption and triggers a PHP 
2,000 per unit tuition fee11. 
●​ Accumulation of unofficially dropped (UD) courses exceeding institutional limits7.
