Valenzuela City Dr. Pio Valenzuela Scholarship 
Program (ID: 33) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Valenzuela City Dr. Pio Valenzuela Scholarship Program13 
●​ Provider: Valenzuela City Government / City Social Welfare and Development Office13 
●​ Category: Local Government Unit (LGU) / Merit-and-Need3 
●​ Official Website: https://www.valenzuela.gov.ph13 
●​ Application Portal: https://www.valenzuela.gov.ph/drpioscholarship13 
●​ Official Guidelines: Valenzuela Municipal Council Ordinance No. 12 of 1995 (amended by 
City Ordinance No. 37, Series of 2009)13 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 


--- PAGE 29 ---
To grant annual financial assistance to underprivileged but academically competent high 
school graduates in Valenzuela City pursuing baccalaureate degree programs in 
CHED-accredited public or private higher education institutions13. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born Filipino Citizen13. 
●​ Residency: Long-term resident of Valenzuela City (at least four consecutive years of 
residency immediately prior to application)13. 
●​ Education Level: College / Undergraduate (Incoming Freshmen)13. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1 (Incoming First-Year College Students)13. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: Yes13. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible for new entry13. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible13. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Enrolled or applying for admission in any 
CHED-accredited college or university13. 
●​ Academic Requirements: SHS GWA of at least 85.00% with no subject grade below 85% 
in Grade 11 (1st and 2nd sem) and Grade 12 (1st sem)13. Must pass the scholarship 
qualifying examination13. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 85.00% (with zero subject grades below 85%)13. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined gross annual income of employed parents must NOT exceed 
PHP 120,000.0013. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ School Restrictions: Any public or private HEI accredited by the Commission on Higher 
Education (CHED)13. 
●​ Priority Courses: Open to all baccalaureate degree programs under GAS, STEM, HUMSS, 
and ABM strands17. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Underprivileged/low-income family status verified via BIR ITR or 
Barangay Certificate of Indigency13. 
●​ Good Moral: Required (Certificate of Good Moral Character)13. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must be a graduate of a public or private senior high school in 
Valenzuela City13. Voter's certificate of parent/applicant required16. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Graduating Grade 12 students and SHS graduates residing in Valenzuela 
City for >= 4 years13. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes (prior to/at starting college intake)13. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: No13. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: No13. 


--- PAGE 30 ---
●​ Can current seniors apply?: No13. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No13. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: No13. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: January 3 annually13. 
●​ Closing Date: Late February / Mid-March (e.g., March 20)13. 
●​ Qualifying Exam Date: Mid-March / April annually13. 
●​ Application Cycle: Fixed / Annual3. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–20273. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Educational grant allocation13. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: Integrated into annual grant13. 
●​ Allowance: PHP 30,000.00 per academic year (PHP 15,000.00 per semester)13. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into semestral allowance13. 
●​ Transportation: Integrated into semestral allowance13. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Return Service: None13. 
●​ Other Benefits: None13. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Accomplished Dr. Pio Valenzuela Scholarship Application Form14. 
2.​ Certified True Copy of Grade 11 Report Card (1st & 2nd Semesters) and Grade 12 Report 
Card (1st Semester) showing GWA >= 85% and no grade below 8516. 
3.​ Proof of Income: Certified True Copy of 2024 ITR (Form 2316) showing annual gross 
income <= PHP 120,000.00, or Joint Affidavit and Certificate of Non-Filing of ITR if 
unemployed16. 
4.​ Barangay Certificate of Residency and Indigency of parents16. 
5.​ PSA Certified Birth Certificate of applicant16. 
6.​ Certificate of Good Moral Character16. 
7.​ Voter's Certificate of registered parent or guardian16. 
8.​ Photo of actual street residence of applicant16. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Must maintain a semestral GWA of at least 2.00 (85%)13. 
●​ Regular Load: Full-time credit enrollment13. 


--- PAGE 31 ---
●​ No Failures: Individual subject grades must not fall below 2.2513. 
●​ Return Service: None13. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Parents' annual gross income exceeding PHP 120,000.0013. 
●​ Subject grade below 85% in SHS or below 2.25 in college13. 
●​ Residency in Valenzuela City less than four (4) consecutive years13. 
●​ Non-natural-born citizenship13.

Marikina City Medical Scholarship Program (ID: 35) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Marikina City Medical Scholarship Program3 
●​ Provider: Marikina City Government3 
●​ Category: Local Government Unit (LGU) / Graduate / Return Service Obligation3 
●​ Official Website: https://www.marikina.gov.ph3 
●​ Application Portal: Marikina City Hall / Health & Education Office3 
●​ Official Guidelines: Marikina City Ordinance on Medical Scholarships3 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To address health human resource needs in Marikina City by providing full financial support to 
deserving resident students pursuing a Doctor of Medicine degree in exchange for mandatory 
return service in public health facilities3. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino Citizen3 
●​ Residency: Bona fide resident of Marikina City3. 
●​ Education Level: Graduate (Doctor of Medicine)3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1, 2, 3, 4 (Medical School Years)3. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No3. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Eligible as medical students3. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes (Restricted strictly to Doctor of Medicine degree)3. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Accepted or enrolled in a recognized Doctor of 
Medicine program3. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Bachelor's degree completion and NMAT score meeting 
medical school entry standards; GWA >= 85.00%3. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 85.00%3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined annual gross family income must not exceed PHP 


--- PAGE 38 ---
600,000.003. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE

●​ Priority Courses: Doctor of Medicine3. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Good Moral: Required3. 
●​ Health Requirements: Physically and mentally fit3. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Mandatory return service obligation in Marikina City public 
hospitals/health centers upon passing the Physician Licensure Examination3. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Incoming and ongoing medical students residing in Marikina City3. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes (1st year medical students)3. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes (2nd year medical students)3. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes (3rd year medical students)3. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes (4th year medical students)3. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes (Bachelor's graduates entering medical school)3. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes3. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Summer intake period prior to medical academic year3. 
●​ Closing Date: Announced per annual notice3. 
●​ Application Cycle: Fixed / Annual3. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–20273.

●​ Monthly Stipend: PHP 4,000.00 per month3.

●​ Transportation: Integrated into annual grant3.

●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE

--- PAGE 39 ---

6.​ Income Tax Return of parents (income <= PHP 600,000)3. 
7.​ Signed Return Service Contract3. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Maintain passing GWA prescribed by medical school and scholarship 
rules3.

●​ No Failures: Zero failing grades in medical subjects3. 
●​ Return Service: Mandatory service execution post-graduation3.

Taguig LANI Full Scholarship (ID: 95) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Taguig City L.A.N.I. Full Scholarship Track1

2. Purpose 
To provide full educational financial coverage to the Top 10 graduating students of public 
senior high schools in Taguig City, regardless of the college or course they enroll in, as well as 
students pursuing priority S&T, law, or medical programs1.

●​ Residency: Bona fide resident of Taguig City for at least three (3) years1.

●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No1. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes1. 
●​ Graduate Students?: No (Except Law and Medicine)1.

public SHS graduates, or DOST priority/law/medicine schools1. 
●​ Academic Requirements: GWA of at least 85.00% or designated Top 10 class rank1. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 85.00%1. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: Top 10 graduates of public high schools in Taguig1. 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ School Restrictions: Unrestricted for Top 10 public SHS grads; DOST/PRC/CHED 
accredited for priority tracks1. 
●​ Priority Courses: Unrestricted for Top 10 public SHS grads; DOST S&T, Law, and Medicine 
for other applicants1. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: PDAO endorsement required for PWD applicants1. 
●​ Good Moral: Required1.

●​ Other Official Rules: Voter registration requirement for applicant/parent1.

1. Scholarship Identity

●​ Provider: Taguig City Government / Taguig Scholarship Secretariat1 
●​ Category: Local Government Unit (LGU) / Graduate / Public & Educator Sectoral1 
●​ Official Website: https://www.taguig.gov.ph1 
●​ Application Portal: https://tcu.edu.ph/lani-scholarship1 
●​ Official Guidelines: City Ordinance No. 9, Series of 2011; LANI LEAD Guidelines1 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To advance human capital and professional leadership by subsidizing Master's and Doctoral 
degree studies for Taguig resident teachers, local/national government employees, and 
uniformed personnel serving in Taguig City1. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino Citizen1 
●​ Residency: Bona fide resident of Taguig City for at least three (3) years immediately 
preceding application1. 
●​ Education Level: Graduate (Master's or Doctoral Degree)1. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1, 2, 3 (Postgraduate Years)1. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No1. 
●​ Existing College Students?: No (Restricted to post-baccalaureate graduate students)1. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes (Primary target cohort)1. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Enrolled in a Master's or Doctoral program with courses 
aligned with applicant's profession1. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Latest work performance rating of "Excellent" or at least "Very 
Satisfactory"1; GWA >= 85.00%3. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 85.00%1. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Age Restrictions: Must NOT exceed 50 years of age at time of application1. 
●​ School Restrictions: Recognized public or private graduate schools1. 
●​ Priority Courses: Graduate degree courses strictly aligned with applicant's current 
profession1. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Must have been in service for at least three (3) years in a national 
or local government office in Taguig, or a teacher in a public/private school in Taguig, or 
uniformed PNP personnel based in Taguig1. 
●​ Good Moral: Required (Good moral character in paper and deeds)1. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Other Official Rules: DepEd teachers require endorsement from DepEd Superintendent;

1. Scholarship Identity

●​ Official Guidelines: Makati City Ordinance No. 2019-A-0366 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To incentivize high-performing Makati public senior high school graduates to pursue higher 
education in top private universities and colleges in Metro Manila that have been officially 
designated as Centers of Excellence (COE) by CHED6. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino Citizen6 
●​ Residency: Bona fide resident of Makati City6. 
●​ Education Level: College / Undergraduate3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1 (Incoming Freshmen)6. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: Yes6. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible for initial entry6. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible6. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Enrolled or accepted as an incoming 1st-year student in 
a private college or university in Metro Manila declared a CHED Center of Excellence6. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Fresh senior high school graduate belonging to the Top 10 
Percent of the graduating class6. 
●​ Minimum GWA: Minimum GWA of 1.50 (or equivalent 88.00% scale)3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: Belong to the Top 10% of the SHS graduating class6. 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ School Restrictions: Private colleges and universities in Metro Manila declared as CHED 
Centers of Excellence6. 
●​ Priority Courses: CHED Center of Excellence degree programs6. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Good Moral: Required6. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must sign a mandatory Service Contract Agreement committing to 
serve with the Makati City Government after graduation6. 


--- PAGE 68 ---

top 10% of their class6. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes (at initial college entry)6.

●​ Can current juniors apply?: No6. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: No6. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No6. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: No6.

●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE

University of Makati Special Institutional Scholarship 
(ID: 99) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: University of Makati Special Institutional Scholarship7 
●​ Provider: University of Makati (UMak) / Partner Sponsoring Agencies7 
●​ Category: Institutional / Sectoral & Affiliation3 
●​ Official Website: https://www.umak.edu.ph7 
●​ Application Portal: UMak OLEA Portal 
(https://www.umak.edu.ph/admissions/scholarships/)7 
●​ Official Guidelines: UMak City Ordinance No. 2024-108; UMak Special Scholarship 
Guidelines8 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To extend institutional token fee exemptions and sponsor stipends to designated special 
demographic groups, including dependents of Makati City Government employees, senior 
citizens, PWDs, AFP dependents, DILG-endorsed enrollees, and student-athletes/artists7. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino Citizen7 
●​ Residency: Dependent on sub-track (Makati employees/DILG/OSCA tracks require local 
affiliation; sports/arts tracks open)7. 
●​ Education Level: College / TVET3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1, 2, 3, 4, 53. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No7. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes7. 


--- PAGE 72 ---
●​ Graduate Students?: Restricted7. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Officially enrolled at the University of Makati7. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Passing GWA >= 75.00% (or standard college retention GWA <= 
2.50)3. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 75.00%3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Age Restrictions: Senior Citizen track requires applicant to be at least 70 years of age at 
time of application7. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to students enrolled at the University of Makati7. 
●​ Priority Courses: Any undergraduate or technical-vocational course offered at UMak7. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Must belong to one of the designated categories and present 
official endorsement: 
1.​ Makati City Government Employee Dependent (Human Resource endorsement)11. 
2.​ Senior Citizen (OSCA endorsement + Senior Citizen ID, age >= 70)7. 
3.​ PWD (Center for Inclusive Education endorsement + PWD ID)7. 
4.​ AFP Beneficiary (AFP ID or Certificate)7. 
5.​ Barangay/DILG (DILG Makati endorsement)7. 
6.​ Student-Athlete / Artist (Center for Athletic Development / Center for Culture and 
Arts endorsement under Achievement Scholarship)11. 
●​ Good Moral: Required7. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Other Official Rules: Endorsement letters must be submitted once per academic year or 
per term as mandated7. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Officially enrolled UMak students meeting special sectoral criteria7. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes7. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes7. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes7. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes7. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No7. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes (Requires annual/semestral re-endorsement)7. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Announced semestrally on UMak OLEA system7. 
●​ Closing Date: Term registration deadline12. 
●​ Application Cycle: Semestral3. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–20273. 


--- PAGE 73 ---
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Full (100%) or Partial (50%) exemption from tuition/token fees7. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: PHP 1,000.00 per month (depending on sponsor track)3. 
●​ Allowance: Total value up to PHP 15,000.00 per school year3. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into sponsor grant7. 
●​ Transportation: Integrated into sponsor grant7. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Return Service: Active participation in university sports, culture, or community programs 
(for athletes/artists)7. 
●​ Other Benefits: Complete waiving of token fee assessment on Scan-to-Pay9. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Proof of Enrollment at UMak7. 
2.​ Preceding Term Grade Report7. 
3.​ Specific Sectoral Endorsement / ID: 
○​ DILG Makati Endorsement Letter7. 
○​ OSCA Endorsement + Senior Citizen ID (for age >= 70)7. 
○​ Center for Inclusive Education Endorsement + PWD ID7. 
○​ AFP Beneficiary Certificate / ID7. 
○​ Proof of Parent Employment in Makati City Government11. 
4.​ Online application submission via UMak OLEA Portal7. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Maintain passing GWA and comply with university retention policies7. 
●​ Regular Load: Full academic credit load carried7. 
●​ No Failures: Zero failing grades7. 
●​ Return Service: Submission of renewed annual endorsement letter7. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Failure to submit updated sectoral endorsement letter7. 
●​ Unapproved reduction of academic unit load7.

NavotaAs Fisherfolk Children Scholarship Track (ID: 
100) 


--- PAGE 76 ---
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: NavotaAs Fisherfolk Children Scholarship Track19 
●​ Provider: Navotas City Government / City Agriculture's Office (CAO)19 
●​ Category: Local Government Unit (LGU) / Need-based / Sectoral3 
●​ Official Website: https://www.navotas.gov.ph19 
●​ Application Portal: City Agriculture's Office / Navotas City Hall19 
●​ Official Guidelines: Navotas City Ordinance on Fisherfolk Educational Support19 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To promote socio-economic mobility and guarantee higher education access for the children 
of registered marginalized fisherfolk in Navotas City through dedicated tuition grants and living 
allowances19. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino Citizen19 
●​ Residency: Bona fide resident of Navotas City19. 
●​ Education Level: College / TVET3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1, 2, 3, 43. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No3. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes3. 
●​ Graduate Students?: No3. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Enrolled or accepted in a recognized college, university, 
or technical-vocational institution19. 
●​ Academic Requirements: GWA of at least 78.00%3. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 78.00%3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined annual gross family income must not exceed PHP 
180,000.003. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ School Restrictions: Recognized public or private HEIs or TVET training centers19. 
●​ Priority Courses: Agriculture, Fisheries, Marine Biology, TVET trades, and general degree 
programs19. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Parent must be an officially registered fisherfolk listed in the 
Navotas City Juan Magsasaka / Fisherfolk Database maintained by the City Agriculture's 
Office (CAO)19. 
●​ Good Moral: Required19. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must present official Fisherfolk Identification Card (ID) issued by 


--- PAGE 77 ---
CAO19. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Direct legitimate children or dependents of registered Navotas 
fisherfolk3. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes3. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes3. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes3. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes3. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No3. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes3. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Annual cycle managed by City Agriculture's Office19. 
●​ Closing Date: Announced per annual cycle19. 
●​ Application Cycle: Fixed / Annual3. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–20273.

●​ Transportation: Integrated into annual package3.

●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Return Service: None3.

Canonical Verification Report for 
ISKONNECT Scholarship Database 
This canonical verification report establishes the definitive ground truth for sixteen local 
government unit (LGU) and specialized scholarship programs within the ISKONNECT 
scholarship matching platform1. To prevent false-positive recommendations that direct 
applicants toward ineligible grants and false-negative omissions that conceal viable funding, 
each scholarship record has been audited against Tier 1 primary evidence, including 
government portals, official ordinances, downloadable citizen charters, and governing 
executive orders2. 
Per ISKONNECT verification protocol, fields not explicitly declared in primary source 
documentation are designated as NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE to preserve 
deterministic database mapping2. 
Pasig City Regular Academic Scholarship (ID: 25) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Pasig City Regular Academic Scholarship Program4 
●​ Provider: Pasig City Government (Pasig City Education Department – Scholarships and 
Awards Section / PCED-SAS)5 
●​ Category: Local Government Unit / Merit-and-Need1 
●​ Official Website: https://www.pasigcity.gov.ph1 
●​ Application Portal: https://scholars.pasigcity.gov.ph1 
●​ Official Guidelines: Pasig City Education Department Citizen's Charter5 
●​ Current Status: Active1 
2. Purpose 
The Pasig City Regular Academic Scholarship Program expands educational access for 
bonafide Pasig City residents across basic and tertiary education levels5. By offering semestral 
financial grants to students from low-to-middle-income families, the program seeks to alleviate 
household financial pressure while encouraging high academic achievement in public and 
private institutions5. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino citizen3 
●​ Residency: Bonafide resident of Pasig City5 
●​ Education Level: Elementary, Junior High School, Senior High School, College4 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: All year levels (Grade 1 through College Senior)1 


--- PAGE 2 ---
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No7 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes7 
●​ Graduate Students?: No1 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Enrolled in a recognized public or private educational 
institution4 
●​ Academic Requirements: Passing general weighted average as certified by report of 
grades5 
●​ Minimum GWA: 85.00%1 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined family gross annual income not exceeding PHP 300,000.00 
or submission of a Barangay Certificate of Indigence1 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ School Restrictions: Recognized public or private schools5 
●​ Priority Courses: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Good Moral: Certificate of Good Moral Character8 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Other Official Rules: Strict enforcement of the "One-Scholar-One-Family Policy" (only 
one scholar per household)6; Voter's Certification Record (VCR) of parent (for minors) or 
student (if 18+)8. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Resident students in elementary, secondary, and tertiary levels4 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes7 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes7 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes7 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes7 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No1 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes (via annual renewal/reenlistment)7 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: August 1, 20251 
●​ Closing Date: September 19, 20257 
●​ Application Cycle: Annual1 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–20264 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Covered up to city cap for private school scholars; free in SUCs/LUCs1 
●​ Monthly Stipend: PHP 1,500.00 per month1 
●​ Allowance: Total annual value up to PHP 25,000.001 


--- PAGE 3 ---
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into general stipend1 
●​ Transportation: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: Available under separate PCED-SAS board review grant8 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Return Service: False1 
●​ Other Benefits: Priority access to city youth development activities5 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Printed Online Scholarship Application Form5 
2.​ School ID (photocopy, front and back)5 
3.​ Report of Grades / Card for the preceding academic year4 
4.​ Proof of Enrollment / Enrolment Slip for current academic year4 
5.​ Proof of Parents' / Guardians' Income (ITR, Pay Slip, or Barangay Certificate of Indigence)4 
6.​ Barangay Certificate of Residency with years of residence indicated4 
7.​ Voter's Certification Record (VCR) of parent or applicant8 
8.​ Written Essay: "Why do I want to be a Pasig Scholar?"4 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: 85.00%1 
●​ Regular Load: Full-time academic load5 
●​ No Failures: No failing or dropped subjects5 
●​ Return Service: None1 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Sibling already enjoying a Pasig City scholarship (violating One-Scholar-One-Family 
rule)6. 
●​ Non-residency in Pasig City5. 
●​ Annual family income exceeding PHP 300,000.00 without indigency status1.

1. Scholarship Identity

2. Purpose 
The Quezon City Scholarship Program provides educational grants to underprivileged, 
academically qualified, and sectorally deserving Quezon City residents2. Enacted under 
Ordinance No. SP-3283, S-2024, it encompasses multiple sub-category tracks across Senior 
High School, Tertiary, TVET, and Postgraduate levels to ensure inclusive educational support2. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino citizen2 
●​ Residency: Bona fide resident of Quezon City holding a valid QCitizen ID2 
●​ Education Level: Senior High School, College, TVET, Postgraduate2

●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No (Varies: QC Excel is incoming 1st year; 
Academic/Economic cover ongoing)2 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes2 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes (Postgraduate Scholarship Track)2 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Enrolled, registered, or accepted in an educational 
institution recognized by the city2 
●​ Academic Requirements: Academic Track: GWA 
 1.75 (89.00%) or SHS Academic 
Honors 1–10; Economic Track: GWA 
 3.00 (75.00%); Athletic/Arts & Youth Leaders 
Tracks: GWA 
 2.50 (85.00%)2

●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE

●​ Current Status: Active1 
2. Purpose 
Established under Ordinance No. SP-3458, S-2025, this specialized scholarship program 
promotes the national language, cultural preservation, and literary production17. It provides 
financial assistance and publication grants to tertiary students, postgraduate researchers, 
educators, and creative writers specializing in Filipino language, literature, translation, and 
Philippine studies18. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements

Davao City STEP FA Category C (ID: 102) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Scholarship on Tertiary Education Program – Financial Assistance 
(STEP-FA) Category C1 
●​ Provider: Davao City Government (EBSU)11 
●​ Category: Local Government Unit / Need-based1 
●​ Official Website: https://davaocity.gov.ph1 
●​ Application Portal: https://ebsu-escholar.davaocity.gov.ph11 
●​ Official Guidelines: Executive Order No. 7 s. 2014 / EBSU Need-Based Aid Rules11 
●​ Current Status: Active1 
2. Purpose 
STEP Financial Assistance Category C offers financial aid to underprivileged tertiary students in 
Davao City who maintain a passing GWA between 80.00% and 89.99%12. It expands access to 
basic tertiary subsidies for low-income urban families11. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino citizen3 
●​ Residency: Resident of Davao City13 
●​ Education Level: College1 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Years 1, 2, 3, and 41 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No1 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes12 
●​ Graduate Students?: No1 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Enrolled in a CHED-recognized HEI in Davao City13 
●​ Academic Requirements: GWA of 80.00% to 89.99% with no failing marks12 
●​ Minimum GWA: 80.00%1 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined family annual gross income not exceeding PHP 180,000.001 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ School Restrictions: Recognized HEIs operating within Davao City13 
●​ Priority Courses: CHED-prescribed priority degree courses13 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Verified indigency status by CSWDO12 
●​ Good Moral: Certificate of Good Moral Character13 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Other Official Rules: Strict "One Child Per Family" rule13; candidate must not hold 
duplicate government/private scholarship grants13.

Davao City Medical and Law Education Assistance 
Program (ID: 103) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Medical and Law Education Assistance Program1 
●​ Provider: Davao City Government (EBSU)11 
●​ Category: Local Government Unit / Graduate Professional (Medicine & Law)1 
●​ Official Website: https://davaocity.gov.ph1 
●​ Application Portal: https://ebsu-escholar.davaocity.gov.ph11 
●​ Official Guidelines: Executive Order No. 7 s. 2014 / EBSU Med-Law Rules11 
●​ Current Status: Active1 
2. Purpose 
The Medical and Law Education Assistance Program finances professional medical and legal 
education for deserving Davao City residents11. It addresses the local shortage of public health 
physicians and legal officers by supporting students in Doctor of Medicine and Juris Doctor 
degree programs11. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino citizen3 
●​ Residency: Bonafide resident of Davao City13 
●​ Education Level: Graduate (Doctor of Medicine or Juris Doctor)1 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Medical/Law Years 1, 2, 3, and 41 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No13 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes (as professional medical/law students)13 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes1 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Accepted or enrolled in a Davao City-based medical or 
law school13 
●​ Academic Requirements: Incoming 1st Year: Bachelor's degree average grade of at least 
85.00% with no grade below 75.00% in any subject; Ongoing (2nd–4th Year): Average 
grade of at least 77.00% (or 85.00% per updated EBSU release) with no grade below 
75.00% in preceding year level12 
●​ Minimum GWA: 85.00%1 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined family gross annual income not exceeding PHP 500,000.00;

schools13

●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE 
●​ Good Moral: Certificate of Good Moral Character13 
●​ Health Requirements: Medical Certificate issued by a government hospital confirming 
physical and mental fitness13

grant13; Sworn statement of no parent or sibling as an active city scholar13; Mandatory 
return service obligation in Davao City1.

Production Database Mutation Directives & Migration 
Architecture 
To execute these database updates across the platform, database administrators must run the 
following Data Definition Language (DDL) schema alterations and Data Manipulation Language 
(DML) mutation scripts against public.scholarships1: 
 
 
 
SQL 
-- Extend public.scholarships schema with missing temporal control parameters​
ALTER TABLE public.scholarships​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS eligible_year_levels INT[] DEFAULT '{1}',​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS incoming_year_only BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS requires_current_enrollment BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS renewal_gwa NUMERIC(5,2) DEFAULT NULL,​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS maximum_gwa_cap NUMERIC(5,2) DEFAULT NULL,​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS one_scholar_per_family_clause BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS lgu_exclusivity_clause BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,​


--- PAGE 68 ---
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS sk_endorsement_required BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS als_osl_status_required BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS voter_status_required BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,​
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS sectoral_restriction VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL;​

Systemic Matching Engine Architecture & Operational 
Recommendations 
Executing these database updates and pipeline enhancements aligns the live ISKONNECT 
matching engine with canonical provider rules, mitigating false recommendations and 
presenting Filipino students with accurate matching results2. 
1.​ Local Government Unit Exclusivity Filtering: Programs such as the Quezon City 
Scholarship Program (ID 88) explicitly prohibit dual enjoyment of other LGU grants2. The 
system must evaluate lgu_exclusivity_clause to prevent candidates already holding local 
grants from receiving invalid matches2. 
2.​ Household Concentration Caps: Pasig City's One-Scholar-One-Family Policy (ID 
25–94)6 and Davao City's One-Child-Per-Family Rule (ID 37, 38, 101, 102, 103)13 represent 
strict administrative constraints. The engine must check active sibling grants within the 
user family profile before rendering recommendations6. 
3.​ Dynamic Grade Band Allocation: Davao City's STEP-FA program (Categories A, B, and 
C) divides funding levels based on GWA brackets (
, 
, and 
)12. Implementing maximum_gwa_cap ensures applicants are routed 
to the specific tier that maximizes their financial award12.

Canonical Verification Audit Report: 
ISKONNECT Scholarship Engine (Group 
B Program Audits) 
The ISKONNECT scholarship matching platform operates an automated engine designed to 
process student profile vectors against financial assistance programs across public, 
institutional, and private sectors1. To eliminate false-positive recommendations that direct 
applicants to ineligible programs and false-negative omissions that suppress viable funding, 
this canonical audit establishes the verified ground truth for all Group B database records1. 
Production database updates require deterministic verification: every eligibility parameter must 
be validated against Tier 1 primary evidence, mapped to explicit database columns, and 
reconciled with governing agency mandates1. Where an official provider document or 
governing policy does not explicitly state a requirement, platform protocol mandates 
designating the field as NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE rather than introducing 
unverified assumptions1. 
The data verification framework strictly adheres to a four-tiered authority hierarchy1. Tier 1 
(Highest Authority) comprises direct government agency web portals (.gov.ph), official 
provider portals, downloadable policy guidelines, CHED Memorandum Orders (CMOs), GSIS 
Policy and Procedural Guidelines (PPGs), DOST Administrative Orders, and official application 
portals1. Tier 2 covers official announcements, press releases, and direct advisories published 
by scholarship secretariats1. Tier 3 consists of trusted secondary sources, such as university 
scholarship office notices, which serve solely to locate primary documentation1. Tier 4 
comprises unverified aggregators, blogs, and student forums, which are permanently excluded 
from live database updates1. 
DepEd SHS Voucher Program (ID: 17) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Senior High School Voucher Program (SHS VP)3 
●​ Provider: Department of Education (DepEd) in partnership with the Private Education 
Assistance Committee (PEAC)3 
●​ Category: Government / Basic Education Financial Assistance / Grant3 
●​ Official Website: https://deped.gov.ph5, https://ovp.peac.org.ph3 
●​ Application Portal: https://ovap.deped.gov.ph5 
●​ Official Guidelines: DepEd Order No. 11, s. 2015; DepEd Order No. 46, s. 2015; DepEd 
Order No. 60, s. 2017; DepEd Order No. 16, s. 20203 
●​ Current Status: Active 


--- PAGE 2 ---
2. Purpose 
The Senior High School Voucher Program is an institutionalized mechanism of financial 
assistance enacted under Republic Act No. 10533 (Enhanced Basic Education Act of 2013) and 
Republic Act No. 8545 (Expanded GASTPE Act)4. The program provides state subsidies in the 
form of non-cashable vouchers to qualified Grade 10 completers, enabling them to enroll in 
participating private senior high schools, State Universities and Colleges (SUCs), or Local 
Universities and Colleges (LUCs)3. By expanding student choice and leveraging private sector 
educational capacity through public-private partnerships, the voucher program guarantees the 
constitutional mandate of accessible, quality basic education while reducing overcrowding in 
public secondary schools5. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen5. 
●​ Residency: Resident of the Philippines5. 
●​ Education Level: Senior High School (Grades 11 and 12)4. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Grade 11 (Initial redemption occurs upon entry into Grade 11)4. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: Yes (Applicable to incoming Grade 11 SHS students)4. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible4. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible4. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must be enrolled or accepted in a non-DepEd 
VP-participating Senior High School3. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Successful completion of Grade 10 Junior High School (JHS)4. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Passing Grade 10 completion is 
required)4. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE5. 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Grade 10 completers from public 
JHSs and private JHS Educational Service Contracting [ESC] grantees are automatically 
qualified regardless of income; non-ESC private applicants undergo online screening 
subject to national budget allocations)5. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE5. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted to VP-participating non-DepEd Senior High Schools 
(Private SHSs, SUCs, and LUCs)3. 
●​ Priority Courses: All DepEd-approved SHS Tracks and Strands (Academic, 
Technical-Vocational-Livelihood [TVL], Sports, Arts and Design)4. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: None5. 
●​ Good Moral: Certificate of Good Moral Character issued by the originating Junior High 
School4. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Automatically Qualified Voucher Recipients (QVRs) include Grade 10 


--- PAGE 3 ---
completers from Public JHSs, SUC/LUC JHSs, and ESC grantees in Private JHSs5. 
Non-ESC private JHS completers must apply online via the Online Voucher Application 
Portal (OVAP)5. Vouchers are non-cashable grants disbursed directly to participating host 
schools via Land Bank of the Philippines accounts4. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Non-ESC Grade 10 completers from private junior high schools, and 
learners who completed JHS prior to the current school year re-entering basic 
education5. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes (Incoming Grade 11 SHS students)4. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: No (Grade 12 voucher renewal is automatic for Grade 
11 beneficiaries who maintain passing status)4. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: No4. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: No4. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No4. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: No4. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Announced annually via DepEd Order (typically opens between October 
and January)5. 
●​ Closing Date: Specified in the annual policy guidelines (typically February 28 for online 
submissions)5. 
●​ Application Cycle: Fixed / Annual5. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–20275. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Voucher subsidy disbursed directly to host school (NCR Private SHS: up to 
₱22,500/year; NCR SUC/LUC SHS: ₱17,500/year; Non-NCR Private SHS: up to 
₱17,500/year; Non-NCR SUC/LUC SHS: ₱14,000/year)4. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: None (Direct tuition voucher)5. 
●​ Allowance: None9. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into the voucher value if published tuition fees are lower than 
the voucher cap4. 
●​ Transportation: None5. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: Not Applicable4. 
●​ Research Allowance: None4. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: None4. 
●​ Return Service: None4. 
●​ Other Benefits: Voucher validity spans two consecutive years of continuous basic 


--- PAGE 4 ---
education (Grades 11 and 12)4. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ PSA Certified Birth Certificate4. 
2.​ Grade 10 Report Card (Form 138 / SF9) showing Learner Reference Number (LRN)4. 
3.​ Certificate of Junior High School Completion4. 
4.​ Certificate of Good Moral Character signed by JHS Principal4. 
5.​ ESC Certification Letter issued by JHS Principal via ESC IMS (for ESC grantees) or 
Qualified Voucher Applicant (QVA) Certificate (for online applicants)4. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Promoted to Grade 12 in accordance with DepEd academic progression 
standards4. 
●​ Regular Load: Continuous full-time enrollment in the elected SHS track4. 
●​ No Failures: Passing grades across all enrolled SHS subjects4. 
●​ Return Service: None9. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Dropping out of Senior High School in the middle of an academic year4. 
●​ Transferring to a DepEd Public Senior High School4. 
●​ Failing Grade 11 or retaining Grade 11 academic status4. 
●​ Transferring to a non-participating private Senior High School3.

13. Matching Risks 
●​ Income Parameter Misconfiguration: The live database export records max_income: 
500000 for ID 179. Applying a ₱500,000 income ceiling will incorrectly disqualify public 
school completers and ESC grantees who are automatically entitled to voucher subsidies 
regardless of household income5. 
●​ Level Mismatch: Displaying this program to college undergraduate applicants misdirects 
candidates. The recommendation engine must enforce user.education_level == 'Senior 
High School'4. 
14. Verification Summary 
●​ Verification Status: Verified3. 
●​ Last Verified Date: 2026-08-059. 
●​ Official URLs Checked: https://deped.gov.ph5, https://ovap.deped.gov.ph5, 
https://admin.peac.org.ph4. 
●​ Broken Links: None. 
●​ Overall Confidence Score: 95/100. 
ERC Graduate Fellowship (ID: 20) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Energy Regulatory Commission Graduate Fellowship Program9 
●​ Provider: Energy Regulatory Commission (ERC)9 
●​ Category: Government / Graduate Fellowship / Institutional9 
●​ Official Website: https://erc.gov.ph9 


--- PAGE 7 ---
●​ Application Portal: https://erc.gov.ph9 
●​ Official Guidelines: ERC Fellowship Program Guidelines9 
●​ Current Status: Active 
2. Purpose 
The ERC Graduate Fellowship Program is a specialized state-funded fellowship designed to 
build institutional capacity and technical expertise in energy regulation, electric power 
economics, and energy engineering9. By supporting advanced post-baccalaureate education 
and policy research, the fellowship develops high-level regulatory economists, legal experts, 
and energy engineers to strengthen national energy governance, consumer protection, and 
market regulation9. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born Filipino citizen9. 
●​ Residency: Resident of the Philippines9. 
●​ Education Level: Graduate (Master's or Doctorate)9. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1st Year Master's or Doctoral students9. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No9. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible (Restricted to post-baccalaureate graduate 
students)9. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes9. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Accepted or enrolled in a graduate degree program in 
energy regulation, power engineering, or energy economics at an accredited university9. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Undergraduate General Weighted Average (GWA) of at least 
88.00% or equivalent9. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 88.00%9. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE9. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined gross annual family income must not exceed ₱500,000.009. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE9. 
●​ School Restrictions: CHED-accredited universities offering recognized graduate 
programs in law, economics, or engineering9. 
●​ Priority Courses: Energy Law, Energy Economics, Power Engineering, Public Policy9. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: None9. 
●​ Good Moral: Required9. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must pass the ERC interview and selection board evaluation9. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Qualified Bachelor's degree graduates entering or pursuing graduate 
studies in priority energy disciplines9. 


--- PAGE 8 ---
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: No (Undergraduate freshmen barred)9. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: No9. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: No9. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: No9. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes (Primary eligible cohort)9. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes9. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Announced annually per ERC official advisory9. 
●​ Closing Date: Specified in the annual Call for Candidates9. 
●​ Application Cycle: Annual9. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–20279. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Full tuition and matriculation fee coverage9. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: ₱12,000.00 per month9. 
●​ Allowance: Integrated into monthly stipend9. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into total fellowship grant value9. 
●​ Transportation: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE9. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE9. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: Not Applicable9. 
●​ Research Allowance: Thesis or dissertation grant provided per program guidelines9. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE9. 
●​ Return Service: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE9. 
●​ Other Benefits: Total estimated annual grant value of ₱150,000.009. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Official Graduate Fellowship Application Form9. 
2.​ Official Transcript of Records (TOR) from Bachelor's degree showing GWA >= 88.00%9. 
3.​ Proof of Admission / Enrollment in an approved graduate program9. 
4.​ BIR Income Tax Return or Tax Exemption Certificate (Income <= ₱500,000.00)9. 
5.​ Certificate of Good Moral Character9. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Maintain graduate academic retention GWA specified by ERC (minimum 
88.00% or university passing equivalent)9. 
●​ Regular Load: Enrolled in required graduate unit load per semester9. 
●​ No Failures: Zero failing or incomplete marks in graduate coursework9. 
●​ Return Service: Compliance with regulatory research output submission9. 


--- PAGE 9 ---
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Undergraduate enrollment status9. 
●​ Combined family gross annual income exceeding ₱500,000.009. 
●​ Undergraduate GWA dropping below 88.00%9. 
●​ Employment in conflicting energy sector enterprises violating regulatory ethics rules9.

NCIP Educational Assistance Program Degree Track 
(ID: 52) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: NCIP Educational Assistance Program (EAP) – Degree Track10 
●​ Provider: National Commission on Indigenous Peoples (NCIP)10 
●​ Category: Government / Need-and-Sectoral / Tribal10 
●​ Official Website: https://ncip.gov.ph12, https://eais.ncip.gov.ph10 


--- PAGE 11 ---
●​ Application Portal: https://eais.ncip.gov.ph10 
●​ Official Guidelines: NCIP Administrative Order No. 1, s. 2022; Indigenous Peoples 
Education and Advocacy Services (IPEAS) Guidelines10 
●​ Current Status: Active 
2. Purpose 
The NCIP Educational Assistance Program is established under Section 30 of Republic Act No. 
8371 (Indigenous Peoples' Rights Act of 1997)13. The program invests in human capital 
development by offering financial grants to deserving Indigenous Cultural Communities / 
Indigenous Peoples (ICCs/IPs) youth pursuing tertiary degree programs10. By removing financial 
barriers to higher education, the EAP empowers indigenous youth to achieve self-reliance, 
develop professional expertise, and actively contribute to community self-governance and 
ancestral domain protection10. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino citizen10. 
●​ Residency: Resident member of a recognized Indigenous Cultural Community / Ancestral 
Domain10. 
●​ Education Level: College / Undergraduate9. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th, and 5th Year14. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No14. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes14. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible under the Degree Track (Covered under separate 
post-graduate assistance provisions)14. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Enrolled or accepted in a State College or University 
(SUC) or CHED-recognized HEI14. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Must maintain a General Weighted Average (GWA) of at least 
80.00% per semester14. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 80.00%14. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE10. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined family annual gross income must not exceed ₱200,000.009. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE10. 
●​ School Restrictions: State Universities and Colleges (SUCs) nearest to the applicant's 
ancestral domain residence are prioritized14. 
●​ Priority Courses: Degree programs aligned with Ancestral Domain Sustainable 
Development and Protection Plans (ADSDPP)14. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Must submit an official Certificate of Confirmation (COC) issued 
by NCIP attesting to genuine IP tribal membership10. 
●​ Good Moral: Required10. 
●​ Health Requirements: Physically and mentally fit to pursue higher education14. 


--- PAGE 12 ---
●​ Other Official Rules: Enrolled in a minimum load of 18 units per semester unless in 
graduating status14. Dual enjoyment of major government scholarship grants is 
prohibited14. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Qualified IP college students (incoming freshmen and ongoing 
upperclassmen)10. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes14. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes14. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes14. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes14. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No14. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes14. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: June 1 annually (via EAIS portal)9. 
●​ Closing Date: August 15 annually9. 
●​ Application Cycle: Fixed / Annual9. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–20279. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Covered via SUC Free Higher Education or subsidized through the IP Education 
Allowance14. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: Integrated into the annual educational allowance14. 
●​ Allowance: ₱20,000.00 per Academic Year (disbursed semestrally at ₱10,000.00 per 
semester)14. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into the annual educational allowance14. 
●​ Transportation: Integrated into the annual educational allowance14. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: Available under NCIP Support and Advocacy Programs (SAP) board 
review grants10. 
●​ Research Allowance: None14. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: None14. 
●​ Return Service: Mandatory service in the scholar's home IP community for a duration 
equal to the scholarship years enjoyed, or book donation to a community library14. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Official Certificate of Confirmation (COC) on Tribe Membership issued by NCIP10. 
2.​ Accomplished EAIS Online Application Form10. 
3.​ Form 138 / SF9 Report Card (for Freshmen) or Official Transcript of Records / Certificate 


--- PAGE 13 ---
of Grades (for Upperclassmen) showing GWA >= 80.00%10. 
4.​ Certificate of Enrollment / Registration Form showing at least 18 enrolled units14. 
5.​ BIR Tax Exemption Certificate, ITR, or Barangay Certificate of Indigency11. 
6.​ Certificate of Good Moral Character10. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Maintain a semester GWA of at least 80.00%14. 
●​ Regular Load: Minimum enrollment of 18 units per semester14. 
●​ No Failures: Zero failing, incomplete, or dropped subjects14. 
●​ Return Service: Mandatory community service commitment14. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Falsification or tampering of NCIP COC or academic records14. 
●​ Semester GWA dropping below 80.00%14. 
●​ Concurrent enjoyment of another major national government scholarship14. 
●​ Unapproved shiftee or transferee status14. 
●​ Dropping below 18 units without prior NCIP approval14.

NCIP Merit Based Scholarship Program (ID: 53) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: NCIP Merit-Based Scholarship Program (MBSP)10 
●​ Provider: National Commission on Indigenous Peoples (NCIP)10 
●​ Category: Government / Merit-and-Sectoral / Tribal10 
●​ Official Website: https://ncip.gov.ph12, https://eais.ncip.gov.ph10 
●​ Application Portal: https://eais.ncip.gov.ph10 
●​ Official Guidelines: NCIP Administrative Order No. 1, s. 2022; IPEAS Guidelines10 
●​ Current Status: Active 
2. Purpose 
The NCIP Merit-Based Scholarship Program provides competitive scholarship funding to 
academically outstanding Indigenous Peoples students pursuing priority undergraduate degree 
programs10. The program develops high-performing indigenous scholars into technical experts, 
community leaders, and advocates for indigenous rights and sustainable development10. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino citizen10. 
●​ Residency: Resident member of an Indigenous Cultural Community10. 


--- PAGE 16 ---
●​ Education Level: College / Undergraduate9. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th, and 5th Year14. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No14. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes14. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible under MBSP undergraduate track14. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Enrolled or accepted in an SUC or recognized HEI14. 
●​ Academic Requirements: General Weighted Average (GWA) of at least 85.00% per 
semester14. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 85.00%14. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE10. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined family gross annual income must not exceed ₱300,000.009. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE10. 
●​ School Restrictions: SUCs or top accredited private HEIs14.

●​ Good Moral: Required10. 
●​ Health Requirements: Physically and mentally fit14. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Enrolled in full semester load (at least 18 units)14. 
4. Application Timing

●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes14. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes14. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes14. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No14.

Department of Agriculture ACEF GIAHEP SUC Track 
(ID: 55) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Agricultural Competitiveness Enhancement Fund – Grants-in-Aid Higher 
Education Program (ACEF-GIAHEP) SUC Track16 
●​ Provider: Department of Agriculture (DA) in coordination with CHED16 
●​ Category: Government / Need-and-Sectoral / Agriculture16 
●​ Official Website: https://da.gov.ph9 
●​ Application Portal: Integrated via participating State Universities and Colleges (SUCs) and 
DA Regional Field Offices16. 
●​ Official Guidelines: DA-CHED Joint Memorandum Circular No. 2, s. 2024; Republic Act 
No. 10848 (ACEF Law)16 
●​ Current Status: Active 
2. Purpose 
The ACEF-GIAHEP program is established pursuant to Republic Act No. 10848 to protect and 
enhance the competitiveness of the agricultural sector16. The program provides grants-in-aid 
to legitimate children of small farmers and fisherfolk pursuing undergraduate degrees in 


--- PAGE 20 ---
agriculture, forestry, fisheries, and veterinary medicine offered by State Universities and 
Colleges16. By upgrading human capital in agrarian communities, the program fosters 
agricultural modernization, food security, and rural development16. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino citizen16. 
●​ Residency: Resident of the Philippines16. 
●​ Education Level: College / Undergraduate9. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Freshmen and ongoing undergraduate students (Years 1 to 4/5)16. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No16. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes16. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible16. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Enrolled in an eligible agriculture-related degree 
program at a participating SUC16. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Minimum GWA of 75.00% or passing academic grade9. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 75.00%9. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined gross annual income of parents must not exceed 
₱200,000.009. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to State Universities and Colleges (SUCs)16. 
●​ Priority Courses: Agriculture, Agricultural Engineering, Agribusiness, Forestry, Fisheries, 
Veterinary Medicine16. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Parent must be a registered small farmer or fisherfolk listed in the 
Registry System for Basic Sectors in Agriculture (RSBSA) or certified by DA/LGU16. 
●​ Good Moral: Required. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Cannot enjoy another government scholarship covering the same 
expenditure items16. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Children of RSBSA-registered small farmers and fisherfolk entering or 
enrolled in SUC agriculture programs16. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes16. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes16. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes16. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes16. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No16. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes16. 


--- PAGE 21 ---
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Announced per academic cycle16. 
●​ Closing Date: August 15 annually9. 
●​ Application Cycle: Annual9. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–20279. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Covered by SUC Free Higher Education or subsidized16. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: Integrated into semestral grant9. 
●​ Allowance: ₱30,000.00 per Academic Year (₱15,000.00 per semester)9. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into annual allowance9. 
●​ Transportation: Integrated into annual allowance9. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: None16. 
●​ Research Allowance: None16. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: None16. 
●​ Return Service: None9.

2.​ PSA Birth Certificate of applicant16.

4.​ BIR Tax Exemption Certificate or ITR showing family income <= ₱200,000.009. 
5.​ Certificate of Enrollment in an approved SUC agriculture degree program16. 
6.​ Certificate of Good Moral Character. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Maintain passing academic GWA per semester (75.00% or SUC retention 
passing mark)9. 
●​ Regular Load: Full credit load per term16. 
●​ No Failures: Compliance with SUC retention rules16. 
●​ Return Service: None9.

●​ Parent not registered in RSBSA or non-farming status16.

AFPEBSO DND CHED PASUC Scholarship (ID: 56) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: DND-CHED-PASUC Scholarship Program17 
●​ Provider: Armed Forces of the Philippines Educational Benefit System Office (AFPEBSO), 
Department of National Defense (DND), CHED, PASUC17 


--- PAGE 24 ---
●​ Category: Government / Military Dependent / Affiliation17 
●​ Official Website: https://afpebs.ph, https://www.afp.mil.ph9 
●​ Application Portal: AFPEBSO Central Office & Regional AFPEBSO Offices17. 
●​ Official Guidelines: CHED Memorandum Order (CMO) No. 22, s. 2004 (IRR of 
DND-CHED-PASUC Scholarship Program signed March 22, 2004)17 
●​ Current Status: Active 
2. Purpose 
The DND-CHED-PASUC Scholarship Program provides tertiary educational benefits to 
legitimate children of military personnel, contributing to the welfare of armed forces 
members17. The program prioritizes children of soldiers Killed in Action (KIA) or permanently 
disabled due to combat injuries (Complete Disability Discharged Combat-Related [CDD-CR]), 
ensuring access to higher education in State Universities and Colleges17. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born Filipino citizen17. 
●​ Residency: Resident of the Philippines17. 
●​ Education Level: College / Undergraduate (Baccalaureate degrees only; post-graduate 
excluded)17. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th, and 5th Year17. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No17. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes17. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible17. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Admitted or enrolled in a State University or College 
(SUC)17. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Minimum GWA of 80.00% or compliance with host SUC 
admission standards9. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 80.00%9. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Selection prioritized by military 
operational causality status: KIA/CDD-CR first, active personnel second)17. 
●​ Age Restrictions: Dependent child must be below 21 years of age at the beginning of the 
school year17. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to State Universities and Colleges (SUCs)17. 
●​ Priority Courses: Any baccalaureate degree course offered by SUCs17. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Must be a legitimate child of active, KIA, or CDD-CR AFP military 
personnel17. 
●​ Good Moral: Required17. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Other Official Rules: National quota of 200 scholarship slots17. Candidates must satisfy 


--- PAGE 25 ---
SUC admission standards on or before April 1017. Must sign AFPEBSO Certificate of 
Undertaking17. Unapproved transfer between SUCs or leaves of absence strictly 
prohibited17. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Dependents of KIA, CDD-CR, or active military personnel under 21 years 
old17. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes17. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes17. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes17. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes17. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No17. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes17. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Annual application processing opens early in the calendar year17. 
●​ Closing Date: April 20 annually for document processing; Central Scholarship Board 
confirmation before May 1517. 
●​ Application Cycle: Annual17. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–20279. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: 100% Tuition fee waiver covered by the host State University or College (SUC) for 
the entire course duration17. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: Integrated into AFPEBSO annual stipend17. 
●​ Allowance: AFPEBSO provides direct scholar stipend of ₱8,000.00 annually (₱4,000.00 
per semester)17. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into stipend17. 
●​ Miscellaneous Fees: CHED covers payment of miscellaneous fees up to ₱2,500.00 per 
semester (₱5,000.00/year)17. 
●​ Transportation: None17. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: None17. 
●​ Research Allowance: None17. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: None17. 
●​ Return Service: None9. 
●​ Other Benefits: Combined financial award value equals full SUC tuition waiver + 
₱5,000.00/yr misc fees + ₱8,000.00/yr stipend9. 
7. Required Documents 


--- PAGE 26 ---
1.​ Duly accomplished AFPEBSO Application Form with two 2x2 photos17. 
2.​ Letter of Recommendation from DND-CHED-PASUC Central Scholarship Board17. 
3.​ Military Service Record of parent / Casualty Report (KIA or CDD-CR Order) issued by 
OTAG AFP17. 
4.​ PSA Marriage Certificate of parents and PSA Birth Certificate of applicant17. 
5.​ Transcript of Records / Report Card showing GWA >= 80.00%9. 
6.​ Certificate of Good Moral Character17. 
7.​ Signed AFPEBSO Certificate of Undertaking17. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Maintain passing GWA per SUC academic retention rules (minimum 
80.00%)9. 
●​ Regular Load: Enrolled in full-time baccalaureate curriculum load17. 
●​ No Failures: Zero failing marks17. 
●​ Attendance: Strict compliance with SUC rules; no unapproved leave of absence17. 
●​ Return Service: None9. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Dependent age reaching or exceeding 21 years old at the start of the SY17. 
●​ Unapproved transfer to another HEI or taking leave of absence without CSB approval17. 
●​ Academic failure or dismissal from host SUC17. 
●​ Enrollment in private universities or post-graduate degree programs17.

DSWD AICS Educational Assistance (ID: 57) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: DSWD Assistance to Individuals in Crisis Situations (AICS) Educational 
Assistance19 
●​ Provider: Department of Social Welfare and Development (DSWD)19 
●​ Category: Government / Emergency Financial Aid / Social Protection19 
●​ Official Website: https://www.dswd.gov.ph9 
●​ Application Portal: DSWD Regional Field Offices and Community Crisis Intervention 
Sections (CIS)19. 
●​ Official Guidelines: DSWD Memorandum Circular No. 21, Series of 201919 
●​ Current Status: Active 
2. Purpose 


--- PAGE 29 ---
The DSWD AICS Educational Assistance program serves as a social safety net and stop-gap 
measure19. It provides immediate financial aid to students facing acute crisis, death or illness of 
the primary breadwinner, or socio-economic distress, enabling students to meet immediate 
academic expenses and preventing school dropout19. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Filipino citizen19. 
●​ Residency: Resident of the Philippines (verified via Barangay Certificate of Indigency / 
Residency)19. 
●​ Education Level: Grade 11, Grade 12, College / TVET, Graduate9. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: All year levels across secondary, tertiary, and vocational tracks9. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No9. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes9. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Eligible if in verified crisis9. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Enrolled in a recognized educational institution19. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Active student status19. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (No academic grade cutoff 
imposed; crisis evaluation prioritized)9. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined family income within low-income / poverty threshold levels 
(₱150,000.00 cap in live DB export)9. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: None (Public or Private recognized institutions)19. 
●​ Priority Courses: Any course or field of study19. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Target crisis categories: Breadwinner deceased, incapacitated, 
unemployed, or displaced; child of solo parent; child of OFW in distress; 4Ps 
beneficiary19. 
●​ Good Moral: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Evaluated via DSWD Social Worker Case Assessment19. Grant 
provided as a one-time emergency assistance per academic year / crisis event19. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Students in crisis or financially distressed breadwinners19. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes9. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes9. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes9. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes9. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes9. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes (Subject to DSWD crisis re-evaluation guidelines)19. 


--- PAGE 30 ---
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Year-round / Rolling intake9. 
●​ Closing Date: December 31 annually (or subject to annual budget allocation)9. 
●​ Application Cycle: Rolling / Emergency assistance9. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–20279. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: None (Direct cash financial assistance paid to client)19. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: None19. 
●​ Allowance: Outright cash assistance grant up to ₱4,000.00 max depending on level 
(College/Vocational/Graduate: up to ₱4,000.00; SHS: up to ₱3,000.00; JHS: up to 
₱2,000.00; Elementary: up to ₱1,000.00)9. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into cash grant19. 
●​ Transportation: Integrated into cash grant or provided as separate AICS transport 
assistance19. 
●​ Insurance: None19. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: None19. 
●​ Research Allowance: None19. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: None19. 
●​ Return Service: None9. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Certificate of Enrollment / Registration Form or School ID19. 
2.​ Barangay Certificate of Indigency / Residency19. 
3.​ Valid Government ID of applicant or parent/guardian19. 
4.​ DSWD Social Worker Intake and Assessment Sheet19. 
5.​ Specific Crisis Proof (Death Certificate of breadwinner, Medical Certificate, Notice of 
Termination, OFW Distress Report)19. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: None (One-time assistance per crisis evaluation)19. 
●​ Regular Load: Active enrollment19. 
●​ Return Service: None9. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Non-indigent status or family income exceeding threshold19. 
●​ Falsification of crisis documents or barangay indigency certificates19. 
●​ Failure to pass DSWD social work case interview19. 


--- PAGE 31 ---

  "renewable": false,​
  "first_time_only": false,​
  "needs_manual_review": true​
}​
 
13. Matching Risks 
●​ Program Type Misclassification: ID 57 is an emergency cash assistance grant, NOT a 
multi-year academic scholarship19. The matching engine must tag it as 
is_emergency_grant: true to prevent students from confusing it with continuous 
multi-year college scholarships19. 
14. Verification Summary 
●​ Verification Status: Verified19. 
●​ Last Verified Date: 2026-08-059. 
●​ Official URLs Checked: https://www.dswd.gov.ph9, https://fo1.dswd.gov.ph19. 
●​ Broken Links: None. 
●​ Overall Confidence Score: 90/100. 
Bagong Pilipinas Merit Scholarship Program (BPMSP) – 
TVET Track (ID: 77) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Bagong Pilipinas Merit Scholarship Program (BPMSP) – Technical 
Education and Skills Development Authority (TVET) Diploma Track20 
●​ Provider: Technical Education and Skills Development Authority (TESDA) in joint 
partnership with CHED and DepEd20 
●​ Category: Government / National / Merit-based / Technical-Vocational20 


--- PAGE 33 ---
●​ Official Website: https://bpms.ched.gov.ph9, https://tesda.gov.ph25 
●​ Application Portal: https://bpms.ched.gov.ph9 
●​ Official Guidelines: CHED-DepEd-TESDA Joint Memorandum Circular (JMC) No. 1, Series 
of 202620 
●​ Current Status: Active 
2. Purpose 
Established pursuant to Republic Act No. 7796 (TESDA Act of 1994) and General Appropriations 
Act FY 2026 funding directives, the BPMSP TVET Diploma Track supports high-performing 
Senior High School graduates pursuing priority technical-vocational diploma programs offered 
by TESDA-registered Technical Vocational Institutions (TVIs)20. By supporting 
technical-vocational education in high-demand fields, the program bridges critical workforce 
gaps, addresses hard-to-fill occupations, and drives national industrial productivity20. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen24. 
●​ Residency: Resident of the Philippines24. 
●​ Education Level: Technical Education and Skills Development Authority (TVET) Diploma 
level9. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: 1st Year (Incoming first-time entrants into TVET diploma programs)20. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: Yes20. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible (Must have earned zero college or tertiary units)21. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible21. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Accepted or enrolled in a priority diploma program 
offered by a TESDA-registered Technical Vocational Institution (TVI)20. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Grade 12 Senior High School General Weighted Average (GWA) 
of at least 90.00% or equivalent21. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 90.00%21. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE for TVET track (HE track 
uses Top 5 / 95% GWA, TVET track mandates GWA >= 90%)21. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined annual gross income of parents/guardians must not exceed 
₱2,000,000.0020. 
●​ Age Restrictions: No age limit imposed (Irrespective of age provided candidate has 
earned no tertiary units)21. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted to TESDA-registered Technical Vocational Institutions 
(TVIs) delivering approved priority diploma programs20. 
●​ Priority Courses: TESDA-identified priority diploma courses in key growth sectors20. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Special equity groups (PWDs, Solo Parents, IPs, Senior Citizens, 
first-generation students) receive 10 additional ranking points in selection scoring20. 
●​ Good Moral: Required21. 


--- PAGE 34 ---
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Applicants must NOT previously hold a technical-vocational diploma 
or NC Level III or higher (unless acquired as part of the SHS curriculum)21. TESDA has sole 
authority and exclusive jurisdiction over the TVET Diploma Track21. Transferees or shiftees 
with credited tertiary units are ineligible26. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Graduating SHS Grade 12 students, prior SHS graduates with no tertiary 
units, and 4th year HS graduates from SY 2015–2016 or earlier with no tertiary units21. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes (Incoming 1st year TVET diploma students)21. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: No21. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: No21. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: No21. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No21. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: No21. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Announced per annual cycle (portal opens upon JMC call)20. 
●​ Closing Date: June 30, 2026 (for initial cycle)20. 
●​ Application Cycle: Fixed / Annual20. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2026–202720. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Tuition subsidy up to ₱70,000.00 per academic year20. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: Integrated into annual living stipend20. 
●​ Allowance: Living stipend of ₱40,000.00 per academic year20. 
●​ Book Allowance: Book and connectivity allowance of ₱10,000.00 per academic year20. 
●​ Transportation: Integrated into allowance package20. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Research Allowance: None20. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: None20. 
●​ Return Service: Mandatory 1 year of return service in the Philippines for every 1 year of 
scholarship received, prioritizing public and government institutions20. 
●​ Total Annual Package Value: Up to ₱120,000.00 annually20. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Accomplished Online Application Form on official BPMSP portal21. 
2.​ PSA Certified Birth Certificate21. 
3.​ Certified True Copy of Learner's Progress Report Card (Form 138 / SF9) showing SHS 


--- PAGE 35 ---
GWA >= 90.00%21. 
4.​ Proof of Family Income (BIR Tax Exemption Certificate, BIR Form 2316 / 1701, DSWD 4Ps / 
Indigency Certificate, or OFW Employment Contract <= ₱2,000,000.00)20. 
5.​ Proof of Admission / Acceptance to a TESDA-registered TVI Diploma Program 
(Certificate of Acceptance, Training Agreement, or Enrollment Confirmation)21. 
6.​ Signed Parent / Legal Guardian Certification (Annex L)24. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Must pass all subjects every semester to retain grant20. 
●​ Regular Load: Full-time credit enrollment in TVET diploma curriculum21. 
●​ No Failures: Zero failing marks allowed20. 
●​ Return Service: Execution of return service agreement20. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Earning tertiary or college units prior to award21. 
●​ Holding an NC Level III, NC Level IV, or prior TVET diploma (unless part of SHS 
curriculum)21. 
●​ Combined parental gross annual income exceeding ₱2,000,000.0020. 
●​ Failing any subject during the TVET diploma program20. 
●​ Transferees or shiftees with credited tertiary units26.

●​ Track and Level Confusion: Database ID 77 represents the TVET Track of BPMSP, whereas 
ID 76 represents the CHED Higher Education Track9. The system must enforce a minimum 
GWA of 90.00% for ID 77 (versus 95.00% / Top 5 for ID 76) and restrict ID 77 strictly to 
TVET diploma programs20.
