Mindanao State University SASE Academic 
Scholarship (ID: 46) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Mindanao State University System Admission and Scholarship Examination 
(MSU-SASE) Academic Scholarship3 
●​ Provider: Mindanao State University System3 
●​ Category: Institutional / System-wide / Merit-based3 
●​ Official Website: https://sase.msuiit.edu.ph/ / https://www.msumain.edu.ph3 
●​ Application Portal: Integrated into the MSU-SASE Online Application System27 
●​ Official Guidelines: MSU System SASE Scholarship Regulations and Policy Manual27 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To attract high-achieving high school graduates across Mindanao, Sulu, and Palawan 
(MINSUPALA) by awarding merit scholarships to top performers in the annual MSU System 
Admission and Scholarship Examination27. 


--- PAGE 41 ---
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen5. 
●​ Residency: Resident of regions covered by the MSU System (BARMM, Regions IX, X, XI, 
XII, XIII, CARAGA, and Palawan)3. 
●​ Education Level: College (Undergraduate entry)3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Year 1 (Incoming Freshmen)3. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: Yes27. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible27. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible27. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must take the MSU-SASE and qualify for admission into 
any MSU campus (Main Marawi, IIT, Gensan, Maguindanao, Naawan, Sulu, Tawi-Tawi, 
Buug)27. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Top score ranking in the annual MSU-SASE27. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 85.00% (or equivalent top SASE percentile score)3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: Selected based on national SASE score ranking tiers27. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Uncapped (Merit-based award)3. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to MSU System campuses27. 
●​ Priority Courses: Open across all undergraduate degree programs offered by the MSU 
System27. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Special slots for Indigenous Cultural Communities and 
Bangsamoro constituents27. 
●​ Good Moral: Required27. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must enroll in the MSU campus assigned based on SASE 
qualification27. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Graduating Senior High School students registering for the MSU-SASE27. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: No (awarded strictly upon SASE entry)27. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: No. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: No. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: No. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: No. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: SASE registration opens annually in September/October27. 
●​ Closing Date: SASE registration closes in December/January; exam administered in 


--- PAGE 42 ---
November/February27. 
●​ Application Cycle: Annual. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2026–202727. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: 100% tuition and registration fee waiver at all MSU campuses3. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: Semestral living allowance provided3. 
●​ Allowance: Total annual stipend grant value of PHP 20,000.003. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into annual allowance package3. 
●​ Transportation: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Return Service: None3. 
●​ Other Benefits: Priority campus dormitory accommodation27. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ MSU-SASE Application Form and Exam Permit27. 
2.​ Certified True Copy of Grade 11 and Grade 12 Report Cards27. 
3.​ Certificate of Good Moral Character27. 
4.​ PSA Birth Certificate27. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Maintain required Grade Point Average (GPA) per semester set by MSU 
System policy27. 
●​ Regular Load: Full-time academic credit enrollment27. 
●​ No Failures: Zero failing marks (5.0) in any academic subject27. 
●​ Return Service: None. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Failing to meet SASE cut-off score for academic scholarship tier27. 
●​ Dropping below required semestral GPA retention mark27. 
●​ Transferring to a non-MSU institution27.

PUP Entrance Scholarship Program (ID: 68) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: PUP Entrance Scholarship Program29 
●​ Provider: Polytechnic University of the Philippines (Office of Scholarship and Financial 
Assistance - OSFA)29 
●​ Category: Institutional / State University / Equity-and-Merit3 
●​ Official Website: https://www.pup.edu.ph/studentservices/osfa/29 
●​ Application Portal: PUP OSFA Walk-in / Online Scholarship Portal29 
●​ Official Guidelines: PUP Citizen's Charter; PUP OSFA Entrance Scholarship Guidelines30 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To democratize higher education access by providing formal scholarship recognition, fee 
waivers, and grantor endorsement to top-performing high school graduates and marginalized 
student categories entering PUP29. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen30. 
●​ Residency: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Education Level: College (Undergraduate entry)3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Year 1 (Incoming Freshmen)3. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: Yes30. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible (Upperclassmen apply for Resident Scholarship 
instead)30. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible29. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must pass the PUP College Entrance Test (PUPCET) and 
enroll as a first-year student30. 
●​ Academic Requirements: High scholastic or specialized achievement under recognized 
qualification categories30. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Governed by category 
qualification)30. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: Top 10 bracket of graduating class of at least 500 graduates from 
a public high school30. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Uncapped for academic/artist tracks; indigent income criteria for First 
Gen/Indigent track30. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to the Polytechnic University of the Philippines 
(Main Sta. Mesa and branches/campuses)9. 


--- PAGE 46 ---
●​ Priority Courses: Open across all undergraduate degree programs30. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Encompasses 11 official categories: (1) Public HS Top 10 (>=500 
grads); (2) Cultural Artist; (3) Student Athlete; (4) Creative Media Artist; (5) Campus 
Journalist; (6) Differently-abled / PWD; (7) ALS Graduate; (8) Indigenous Peoples (IP); (9) 
Solo Parent; (10) Sangguniang Kabataan (SK) Official; (11) First Generation / Indigent 
Student30. 
●​ Good Moral: Required (Certificate of Good Moral Character)31. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Scholars receive formal "Entrance Scholar of PUP" certification and 
endorsement to corporate CSR grantors30. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Incoming first-year students qualifying under any of the 11 official 
categories30. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes (during initial entry term)30. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: No. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: No. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: No. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: No. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Concurrent with freshmen enrollment schedule30. 
●​ Closing Date: Specified per enrollment period by PUP OSFA30. 
●​ Application Cycle: Annual. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–202729. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: 100% waiver of tuition and other school fees (under RA 10931 Universal 
Access)30. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Allowance: Endorsement to private corporate/foundation grantors for external stipends29. 
●​ Book Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Transportation: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: Cash awards provided for board exam topnotchers 
post-graduation29. 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Return Service: None3. 
●​ Other Benefits: Issuance of official "Entrance Scholar of PUP" Certificate, talent 


--- PAGE 47 ---
development workshops, priority endorsement to private benefactors30. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Certification from SHS Principal (with dry seal) attesting to category (e.g., Top 10 of 
>=500 grads, Campus Journalist, Cultural Artist)30. 
2.​ Form 138 / Grade 12 Senior High School Report Card31. 
3.​ Certificate of Good Moral Character31. 
4.​ PSA Birth Certificate31. 
5.​ Category Proof (e.g., NCIP Certificate for IP, PWD ID, SK Oath of Office, ALS Rating)12. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Must transition to PUP Resident Scholarship (President's Lister GWA >= 
1.50 or Dean's Lister GWA >= 1.75)33. 
●​ Regular Load: Full-time academic load per term30. 
●​ No Failures: No grade lower than 2.50 in any subject33. 
●​ Return Service: None. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Failure to submit principal's sealed certification of category qualification30. 
●​ Failing grade or mark below 2.50 in any term33. 
●​ Submission of false credentials34.

PUP Student Assistantship Program (ID: 69) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: PUP Student Assistantship Program29 
●​ Provider: Polytechnic University of the Philippines (Office of Scholarship and Financial 
Assistance - OSFA)29 


--- PAGE 50 ---
●​ Category: Institutional / State University / Student Employment / Need-based3 
●​ Official Website: https://www.pup.edu.ph/studentservices/osfa/services33 
●​ Application Portal: PUP OSFA Office (Rm W119) / Submission of Endorsement Form 
(PUP-SAEF-5-OFSS-015)29 
●​ Official Guidelines: PUP Citizen's Charter; PUP Student Assistantship Regulations30 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To provide meaningful work-study financial assistance to regular PUP undergraduate students, 
enabling them to earn hourly compensation by rendering administrative service to university 
offices while completing their degrees30. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen30. 
●​ Residency: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Education Level: College (Undergraduate)3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Years 2, 3, 4, and 530. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No30. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Yes (Requires at least 2nd-year standing)30. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible29. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must be currently enrolled as a regular student with at 
least two semesters (1 year) of residency at PUP30. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Passed all enrolled subjects in the preceding semester30. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Requires 100% passing rate in 
prior term)30. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Income Ceilings: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Targeted at financially needy 
regular students)29. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to PUP Main Campus and constituent branches29. 
●​ Priority Courses: Open across all undergraduate academic programs30. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Good Moral: Required31. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Must be officially endorsed by the head of the PUP 
office/department to be served30. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Regular 2nd, 3rd, 4th, and 5th year PUP undergraduate students30. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: No (Requires at least 2nd-year standing and 1 year 


--- PAGE 51 ---
residency)30. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes30. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes30. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes30. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes (Subject to semestral renewal)30. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: One week after the Adjustment Period of each semester30. 
●​ Closing Date: Set per semestral notice by PUP OSFA30. 
●​ Application Cycle: Semestral renewal. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–202729. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: None (Tuition is already covered under RA 10931)35. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: Hourly compensation of PHP 25.00 / hour31. 
●​ Allowance: Maximum monthly compensation of PHP 2,500.00 (based on max 100 
hours/month)31. 
●​ Book Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Transportation: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Return Service: Work commitment of up to 24 hours per week or 100 hours per month31. 
●​ Other Benefits: Practical administrative work experience within university offices34. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ Student Assistantship Endorsement Form (PUP-SAEF-5-OFSS-015) signed by Office 
Head29. 
2.​ Official Certificate of Registration (Registration Certificate) for current term30. 
3.​ Copy of Grades / Transcript for the preceding semester30. 
4.​ Student Personal Data Sheet29. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Must pass 100% of enrolled units in the preceding semester30. 
●​ Regular Load: Maintain regular student status per semester30. 
●​ No Failures: Zero failing grades in any subject30. 
●​ Return Service: Satisfactory performance of assigned student assistant duties31. 


--- PAGE 52 ---
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Incurring a failing, dropped, or incomplete mark in the preceding term30. 
●​ Irregular student status or year level lower than 2nd Year30. 
●​ Exceeding the maximum limit of 24 work hours per week or 100 hours per month31.

UP College of Law Scholarship Program (ID: 70) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: UP College of Law Scholarship Program17 
●​ Provider: UP College of Law (University of the Philippines Diliman)17 
●​ Category: Institutional / Graduate / Professional Law / Need-and-Equity3 
●​ Official Website: 
https://law.upd.edu.ph/call-for-scholarship-applications-ay-2025-2026/17 
●​ Application Portal: Integrated UP Law Online Scholarship Application System17 
●​ Official Guidelines: UP College of Law Scholarship Announcement and Guidelines17 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To uphold access, diversity, and equity in legal education by supporting Juris Doctor students 
facing economic or personal hardship, providing full financial coverage from Law Aptitude 
Exam (LAE) fee waivers through Bar Examination preparation17. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen12. 
●​ Residency: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Education Level: Graduate (Juris Doctor Program)3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Years 1, 2, 3, and 4 (Juris Doctor)3. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No17. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible (Restricted to law students)17. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Yes (Juris Doctor professional program)17. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must be a bona fide Juris Doctor student at UP College 
of Law17. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Need is the primary selection criterion; academic merit is 
secondary17. 
●​ Minimum GWA: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE (Evaluated holistically based on 


--- PAGE 55 ---
hardship)17. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Evaluated based on Income Tax Return and household asset verification 
form17. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to UP College of Law (Diliman and BGC 
campuses)17. 
●​ Priority Courses: Juris Doctor (JD) degree program17. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Diversity considerations explicitly prioritized: LGBTQIA+ 
individuals, members of Indigenous Peoples (IP) communities, Persons with Disabilities 
(PWDs), single parents, and aspiring first-generation lawyers17. 
●​ Good Moral: Required17. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Applicants must forego other active scholarship awards upon 
acceptance to UP Law Scholarship17. Background checks and home asset inspections are 
conducted17. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: All bona fide Juris Doctor students at UP College of Law17. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes (1st year JD students)17. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes (2nd year JD students)17. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes (3rd year JD students)17. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes (4th year JD students)17. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes (LAE applicants receiving application fee waivers)17. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes17. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: June 30 annually17. 
●​ Closing Date: July 20 annually (Deadlines strictly applied)17. 
●​ Application Cycle: Annual. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–202717. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: 100% waiver of tuition and miscellaneous fees17. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: PHP 10,000.00 per month for 3 to 4 months per semester (Tier 1 & Tier 
2 scholars)17. 
●​ Allowance: Internet and book allowance set at PHP 10,000.00 per semester17. 
●​ Book Allowance: Included in semestral internet/book allowance package17. 
●​ Transportation: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Insurance: Access to UP Health Service benefits16. 


--- PAGE 56 ---
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: Full support for Bar Examination preparation and review fees17.

Ateneo Freshman Merit Scholarship (ID: 104) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Ateneo Freshman Merit Scholarship (AFMS)4 
●​ Provider: Ateneo de Manila University3 
●​ Category: Institutional / University / Premier Merit-based2 
●​ Official Website: https://www.ateneo.edu/college/scholarships/programs6

●​ Official Guidelines: Ateneo Office of Admission and Aid Merit Guidelines6 
●​ Current Status: Active3

honoring the top 50 ACET examinees across local public, private, science, and international 
high schools4.

●​ Residency: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Education Level: College (Undergraduate entry)3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Year 1 (Incoming Freshmen)3. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: Yes4. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible6. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible6.

undergraduate degree program at Ateneo de Manila University6.

Ateneo Magis Scholarship (ID: 105) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: Ateneo Magis Scholarship4 
●​ Provider: Ateneo de Manila University3 
●​ Category: Institutional / University / Full-Ride Need-and-Merit3 
●​ Official Website: https://www.ateneo.edu/college/scholarships/programs6 
●​ Application Portal: Selected from among qualified Ateneo Financial Aid applicants4 
●​ Official Guidelines: Ateneo Office of Admission and Aid Magis Guidelines6 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
Serves as Ateneo de Manila's most comprehensive, all-expenses-paid scholarship, awarded to 
the most financially constrained applicants who demonstrate outstanding academic and 
leadership excellence (selecting 1 scholar each from NCR, Luzon, Visayas, and Mindanao)4. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen5. 
●​ Residency: Applicants are selected across 4 geographic regions: NCR, Luzon, Visayas, 
and Mindanao4. 
●​ Education Level: College (Undergraduate entry)3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Year 1 (Incoming Freshmen)3. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: Yes4.

●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible6.

--- PAGE 64 ---
undergraduate degree program at Ateneo de Manila University6.

high ACET performance4.

●​ Alternative Class Rank: Selected as the top financial aid recipient in the respective 
geographic island group4. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Family gross annual income must demonstrate severe financial 
constraint (capped at PHP 250,000.00)3. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to Ateneo de Manila University6. 
●​ Priority Courses: Open across all 4- and 5-year undergraduate degree programs4.

●​ Book Allowance: Included in learning allowance package4. 
●​ Transportation: Transportation support or round-trip airfare every semester for provincial 
scholars4.

DLSU St La Salle Financial Assistance Grant (ID: 106) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: St. La Salle Financial Assistance Grant21 
●​ Provider: De La Salle University (Office of Admissions and Scholarships)21 


--- PAGE 68 ---
●​ Category: Institutional / University / Need-based3 
●​ Official Website: https://www.dlsu.edu.ph/admissions/scholarships/22 
●​ Application Portal: Integrated DLSU OAS Financial Assistance Online Portal22 
●​ Official Guidelines: DLSU St. La Salle Financial Assistance FAQs & Guidelines AY 
2026–202722 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
To realize De La Salle University's mission of making Lasallian education accessible to 
intellectually capable but financially handicapped Filipino youth through need-based tuition 
waivers and living stipends21. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen21. 
●​ Residency: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Education Level: College (Undergraduate)3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Years 1, 2, 3, 4, and 53. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No22. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Eligible (Separate application calls for current students)22. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible for undergraduate track (Separate St. Mutien Marie Grant 
exists)20. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must pass the DLSU College Admissions Test (DCAT) or 
be an ongoing regular DLSU undergraduate22. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Evaluated based on high school academic competence and 
DCAT score22. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 85.00%3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Structured into 3 tiered income brackets: Bracket A (< PHP 450,000: 
100% tuition + stipend); Bracket B (PHP 450,000–1,000,000: 100% tuition); Bracket C 
(PHP 1,000,001–1,800,000: Partial tuition waiver)22. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to De La Salle University (Manila and Laguna 
campuses)22. 
●​ Priority Courses: Open across all undergraduate degree programs22. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Good Moral: Required22. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Grantees awarded 100% waivers are exempted from paying the PHP 
10,000 reservation fee22. 


--- PAGE 69 ---
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: DCAT examinees, Special DCAT examinees, and ongoing DLSU 
undergraduate students22. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes22. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes22. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes22. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes22. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No22. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes22. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: April 17 for DCAT applicants; May 26 for Special DCAT applicants22. 
●​ Closing Date: May 17 for DCAT applicants; June 1 for Special DCAT applicants (No 
extensions)22. 
●​ Application Cycle: Annual / Semestral. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2026–202722. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Full 100% or partial tuition and fees waiver depending on income bracket22. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: PHP 3,500.00 per month (awarded to Bracket A scholars with family 
income < PHP 450k)3. 
●​ Allowance: Integrated into monthly stipend package22. 
●​ Book Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Transportation: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Return Service: None3. 
●​ Other Benefits: Exemption from PHP 10,000 reservation fee for 100% grant recipients22.

2.​ Parents' Income Tax Return (ITR), BIR Certificate of Tax Exemption, or Employment

--- PAGE 70 ---

DLSU Vaugirard Scholarship Program (ID: 107) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: DLSU Vaugirard Scholarship Program20 
●​ Provider: De La Salle University3 
●​ Category: Institutional / University / Merit-and-Need / Public School Track3 
●​ Official Website: https://www.dlsu.edu.ph/admissions/scholarships/20 
●​ Application Portal: Selected from top DCAT examinees graduating from public/science 
high schools (Screening/interview)20 
●​ Official Guidelines: DLSU OAS Vaugirard Scholarship Guidelines20 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
Exclusively provides full scholarships and living allowances every year to top DCAT examinees 
who graduated from Philippine public and science high schools, promoting social equity and 
public school representation at DLSU20. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen20. 


--- PAGE 73 ---
●​ Residency: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Education Level: College (Undergraduate entry)3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Year 1 (Incoming Freshmen)3. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: Yes20. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Ineligible20. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible20. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must be a graduating student from a Philippine Public 
or Science High School accepted to DLSU20. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Among top examinees in the DLSU College Admission Test 
(DCAT)20. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 88.00% (or top DCAT score equivalent)3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: Selected by University Committee screening from top 
public/science DCAT examinees20. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined family gross annual income must not exceed PHP 
300,000.003. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to graduates of Philippine Public High Schools and 
Science High Schools enrolling at DLSU20. 
●​ Priority Courses: Open across all undergraduate degree programs20. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: Public and Science High School completers20. 
●​ Good Moral: Required20. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Candidates are invited for committee screening; cash incentives are 
awarded to scholars graduating with Latin honors20. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Graduating public and science high school seniors taking the DCAT20. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: No (awarded upon freshman entry)20. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: No. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: No. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: No. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: No. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Automatic screening upon DCAT administration20. 
●​ Closing Date: Committee interviews conducted in April/May prior to confirmation20. 
●​ Application Cycle: Annual. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2026–202722.

●​ Tuition: 100% waiver of tuition, miscellaneous, and laboratory fees throughout stay at 
DLSU20. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: PHP 4,000.00 per month (monthly living stipend)3. 
●​ Allowance: Modest accommodation and allowance coverage20. 
●​ Book Allowance: Integrated into monthly stipend20. 
●​ Transportation: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: Cash incentive awarded for those graduating with Latin honors20. 
●​ Return Service: None3. 
●​ Other Benefits: Advance enrollment privileges20.

1.​ DCAT Application Form and Results20. 
2.​ Public / Science High School Form 138 / Transcript20. 
3.​ Parents' Income Tax Return or BIR Tax Exemption Certificate12. 
4.​ Certificate of Good Moral Character20. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Maintain required trimester GPA retention mark set by OAS24. 
●​ Regular Load: Full-time credit enrollment per trimester20. 
●​ No Failures: Zero failing grades24. 
●​ Return Service: None.

●​ High school origin from a private non-science institution20. 
●​ Parent gross annual income exceeding PHP 300,000.003. 
●​ Failure to maintain trimester GPA retention standard24.

UST San Lorenzo Ruiz Student Assistance Scholarship 
(ID: 108) 


--- PAGE 77 ---
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: UST San Lorenzo Ruiz Student Assistance Scholarship26 
●​ Provider: University of Santo Tomas (Office for Student Affairs)26 
●​ Category: Institutional / University / Student Employment / Need-based3 
●​ Official Website: https://manila.ust.edu.ph/osawebapp/osainfo-scholarshipoffered26 
●​ Application Portal: UST OSA SAAF Portal26 
●​ Official Guidelines: UST Office for Student Affairs San Lorenzo Ruiz Guidelines26 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
Named after Saint Lorenzo Ruiz, the first Filipino saint, this work-study scholarship provides 
financial assistance to UST undergraduate students who render 20 to 30 hours per week of 
student service in university offices26. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen5. 
●​ Residency: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Education Level: College (Undergraduate)3. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: Years 1, 2, 3, 4, and 53. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No26. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Eligible26. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Ineligible26. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must be officially enrolled in an undergraduate degree 
program at the University of Santo Tomas26. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Passing academic record with a General Weighted Average 
(GWA) of at least 82.00% (2.50 UST scale)3. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 82.00%3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Combined family gross annual income must not exceed PHP 
250,000.003. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to the University of Santo Tomas (España, 
Manila)26. 
●​ Priority Courses: Open across all UST faculties, colleges, and institutes26. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Good Moral: Required (Certificate of Good Moral Character from OSA)26. 
●​ Health Requirements: Physically and mentally fit to perform student assistant duties26. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Willingness to render twenty (20) to thirty (30) hours per week of 
service in assigned university offices26. 


--- PAGE 78 ---
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Enrolled UST undergraduate students seeking work-study aid26. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes26. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes26. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes26. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes26. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: No. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes (Semestral renewal)26. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Announced by UST OSA at the start of each semester26. 
●​ Closing Date: Set per semestral notice26. 
●​ Application Cycle: Semestral. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–202726. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: Full or partial tuition and fees discount3. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: Hourly compensation or semestral stipend allowance (total value up to 
PHP 60,000.00/year)3. 
●​ Allowance: Integrated into work-study stipend package3. 
●​ Book Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Transportation: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Return Service: Render 20 to 30 hours per week of service in assigned UST unit26. 
●​ Other Benefits: Development of practical administrative skills26. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ UST OSA SAAF Application Form for San Lorenzo Ruiz Scholarship26. 
2.​ Official Grade Report / Transcript showing GWA >= 82.00%3. 
3.​ Parents' Income Tax Return (ITR) or Barangay Certificate of Indigency26. 
4.​ UST Health Service Medical Clearance26. 
5.​ Endorsement Form from assigned UST office supervisor26. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Maintain a minimum semestral GWA of 82.00% (2.50)3. 
●​ Regular Load: Enrolled in full term credit load26. 


--- PAGE 79 ---
●​ No Failures: Zero failing marks7. 
●​ Return Service: Satisfactory completion of 20–30 weekly service hours26. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Family gross annual income exceeding PHP 250,000.003. 
●​ Failure to render the required 20–30 hours of weekly service26. 
●​ Academic failure or disciplinary violation7.

UST Santo Tomas Academic Scholarship (ID: 109) 
1. Scholarship Identity 
●​ Official Name: UST Santo Tomas Academic Scholarship26 
●​ Provider: University of Santo Tomas (Office for Student Affairs)26 
●​ Category: Institutional / University / Merit-based3 
●​ Official Website: https://manila.ust.edu.ph/osawebapp/osainfo-scholarshipoffered26 
●​ Application Portal: Integrated UST OSA Scholarship Portal / Faculty Dean's Office 
Endorsement26 
●​ Official Guidelines: UST Office for Student Affairs Academic Scholarship Manual26 
●​ Current Status: Active3 
2. Purpose 
Named after Saint Thomas Aquinas, the patron saint of the University, this scholarship awards 
students in Senior High School, College, LEAPMed, Civil Law, and Medicine who demonstrate 
outstanding academic performance26. 
3. Official Eligibility Requirements 
●​ Citizenship: Natural-born or naturalized Filipino citizen5. 
●​ Residency: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Education Level: High School (SHS), College, and Graduate (LEAPMed, Faculty of Civil 
Law, Faculty of Medicine and Surgery)26. 
●​ Eligible Year Levels: All year levels corresponding to the program26. 
●​ Incoming Freshman Only?: No (Covers incoming freshmen Valedictorians/Salutatorians 


--- PAGE 82 ---
and ongoing Dean's Listers)4. 
●​ Existing College Students?: Eligible (Awarded per semester based on Dean's List 
ranking)26. 
●​ Graduate Students?: Eligible (Applicable to Civil Law and Medicine and Surgery)26. 
●​ Current Enrollment Requirement: Must be officially enrolled in UST in Senior High School, 
College, LEAPMed, Law, or Medicine26. 
●​ Academic Requirements: Valedictorian or Salutatorian status for incoming freshmen; Top 
1 or Top 2 rank in the college/faculty for upperclassmen4. 
●​ Minimum GWA: 88.00% (or Dean's List Top 1/Top 2 rank cutoff)3. 
●​ Alternative Class Rank: Rank 1 (100% waiver) or Rank 2 (50% waiver) in the academic 
department/batch4. 
●​ Income Ceilings: Uncapped (Merit-based award independent of income)3. 
●​ Age Restrictions: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ School Restrictions: Restricted strictly to the University of Santo Tomas26. 
●​ Priority Courses: Open across all UST faculties, colleges, institutes, and professional 
schools26. 
●​ Sectoral Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Good Moral: Required26. 
●​ Health Requirements: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Other Official Rules: Application requires certification of academic rank from the High 
School Principal or College Registrar4. 
4. Application Timing 
●​ Who May Apply: Incoming freshmen (Valedictorians/Salutatorians) and ongoing UST 
top-ranked students4. 
●​ Can current freshmen apply?: Yes26. 
●​ Can current sophomores apply?: Yes26. 
●​ Can current juniors apply?: Yes26. 
●​ Can current seniors apply?: Yes26. 
●​ Can graduates apply?: Yes (Civil Law and Medicine students)26. 
●​ Can previous applicants reapply?: Yes (Evaluated every semester based on term rank)26. 
5. Application Window 
●​ Opening Date: Announced by UST OSA at the start of each term26. 
●​ Closing Date: Set per semestral deadline26. 
●​ Application Cycle: Semestral. 
●​ Current AY Covered: AY 2025–2026 / AY 2026–202726. 
6. Benefits 
●​ Tuition: 100% tuition waiver for Rank 1 / Valedictorians; 50% tuition waiver for Rank 2 / 


--- PAGE 83 ---
Salutatorians4. 
●​ Monthly Stipend: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Book Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Transportation: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Insurance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Board Exam Subsidy: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Research Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Graduation Allowance: NOT SPECIFIED IN OFFICIAL SOURCE. 
●​ Return Service: None3. 
●​ Other Benefits: Total estimated annual grant value up to PHP 100,000.003. 
7. Required Documents 
1.​ UST OSA SAAF Application Form for Santo Tomas Academic Scholarship26. 
2.​ High School Principal's Certification of Valedictorian/Salutatorian status (for Freshmen)4. 
3.​ Official Transcript / Registrar Certification of Top 1 or Top 2 Rank in Department (for 
Upperclassmen)26. 
4.​ Certificate of Good Moral Character26. 
8. Renewal Requirements 
●​ Maintain GWA: Must maintain Top 1 or Top 2 academic ranking in the department each 
semester4. 
●​ Regular Load: Enrolled in full term credit load26. 
●​ No Failures: Zero failing grades, 3.0 marks, or incomplete grades7. 
●​ Return Service: None. 
9. Disqualifying Conditions 
●​ Losing Top 1 or Top 2 rank in the academic batch/department4. 
●​ Incurring an incomplete, dropped, or failing grade7. 
●​ Disciplinary sanction issued by UST OSA26.
